function reloadParent(){

	if ("" != "1") {
		return;
	}

	var parentWindow = window.open("", "SIFTFrame", "");

	
	if (!("menuFrame" in parentWindow)) {
		parentWindow.close();
		return;
	}



	var jobListWindow = window.open("", "SYQAA010", "");

	var parentTitle = jobListWindow.document.getElementsByName("displayId");


	if (!parentTitle[0]) {
		jobListWindow.close();
		return;
	}

	
	var parentDisplayID = parentTitle[0].value;
	if (!(parentDisplayID == "SYQAA010" || parentDisplayID == "SYQAA020" || parentDisplayID == "SYQAA030")) {
		return;
	}


	
	if (("reloadMe") in jobListWindow) {

	

	}





}



function isEffectiveDeptOrUserWithOtherProductionBases(userId, deptCd) {
	var keyList = new Array();
	
	keyList.push('6');
	keyList.push('5');
	
	return isEffectiveDeptOrUser(userId, deptCd, keyList);
}










function openWindowFtirProccess(ftirId, proccessCode, proccessCodeExtension) {
	var qs           = '';
	var action       = '';
	var windowName   = '';
	var windowWidth  = '';
	var windowHeight = '';
	
	if ((proccessCode == '110')
			|| (proccessCode == '130')
			|| (proccessCode == '135')
			|| (proccessCode == '137')
			|| (proccessCode == '140')

			|| (proccessCode == '147')

			|| (proccessCode == '150')
			|| (proccessCode == '330')
			|| (proccessCode == '340')
			|| (proccessCode == '500')

			|| (proccessCode == '520')

			) {
		
		
		qs = {
			g1FtirId: ftirId,
			g1ProccessCode: proccessCode,
			g1ProccessCodeExtension: proccessCodeExtension
		};
		action       = '/SYQAA090E00SFnd';
		windowName   = 'SYQAA090' + ftirId;
		windowWidth  = '1008';
		windowHeight = '-1';
		
	} else if ((proccessCode == '120')
			|| (proccessCode == '120')
			|| (proccessCode == '120')) {
		
		qs = {
			g1Id: ftirId
		};
		action       = '/SYQAA110E00SFnd';

		windowName   = 'SYQAA090' + ftirId;
		windowWidth  = '1008';
		windowHeight = '-1';
		
	} else if (proccessCode == '160') {
		
		
		if (proccessCodeExtension == '01') {
			
			qs = {
			   g1Id: ftirId
			};
			action       = '/SYQAA140E00SFnd';

			windowName   = 'SYQAA090' + ftirId;
			windowWidth  = '1008';
			windowHeight = '-1';
			
		} else if ((proccessCodeExtension == '02')
					|| (proccessCodeExtension == '03')) {
			
			var paramActionJudgementCode = '';
			if (proccessCodeExtension == '02') {
				paramActionJudgementCode = '1';
			} else {
				paramActionJudgementCode = '2';
			}
			
			qs = {
				g1Id: ftirId,
				actionJudgementCode: paramActionJudgementCode
			};
			action       = '/SYQAA130E00SFnd';

			windowName   = 'SYQAA090' + ftirId;
			windowWidth  = '1008';
			windowHeight = '-1';
			
		} else {
			
			qs = {
				g1Id: ftirId
			};
			action       = '/SYQAA150E00SFnd';

			windowName   = 'SYQAA090' + ftirId;
			windowWidth  = '1008';
			windowHeight = '-1';
		}
		
	} else {
		return;
	}

	comOpenWindow(action, windowName, qs, windowWidth, windowHeight);
}




function trHighlight(trId, feedback, cons) {

	var trObj = document.getElementById(trId);
	if (trObj.tagName == "A") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement.parentElement;
	} else if (trObj.tagName == "TD") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement;
	}


	var parentcolor;
	if (feedback) {
		parentcolor = window.getComputedStyle(trObj.lastElementChild.previousElementSibling.previousElementSibling).backgroundColor;
	} else {
		parentcolor = window.getComputedStyle(trObj.lastElementChild).backgroundColor;
	}


	parentcolor = rgbtohex(parentcolor);

	var highlightcolor = "#" + darken(parentcolor, -32);

	var beforecolor = "";
	var colorVal = "";
	
	var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
	if (feedback) {
		beforecolor = window.getComputedStyle(elemets[2]).backgroundColor;
	} else {
		beforecolor = window.getComputedStyle(elemets[0]).backgroundColor;
	}
	
	beforecolor = rgbtohex(beforecolor);
	

	
	if (beforecolor == visitedTrHighlightColor) {
		highlightcolor = visitedTrColor;
	}

	if (beforecolor == "" || beforecolor == "#f7f7f7" || beforecolor == "#ffffcc" || beforecolor == "#e5ffe5"
	  || beforecolor == visitedTrColor || beforecolor == visitedTrHighlightColor) { 
		colorVal = highlightcolor;
	} else {
		colorVal = "";
	}


	for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
		if ((feedback) && ((i<2) || (i>elemets.length - 3))) {
		} else {
			elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
		}
	}
	
	elemets = trObj.nextElementSibling.getElementsByTagName("td");
	
	if(window.name.match(/SYQAA0(1|6)0/)){
		for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
			if ((feedback) && ((i<2) || (i>elemets.length - 3))) {
			} else {
				elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
			}
		}
	}	
	
	if (cons) {
		if (document.getElementById(trId + "1")) {
			var trObj = document.getElementById(trId + "1");
			
			var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
			for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
				elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
			}
		}
	} else {
		if (trObj.firstChild.rowSpan) {
			var trRowSpan = trObj.firstChild.rowSpan;
			for (j = 1; j < trRowSpan; j++) {
				if (document.getElementById(trId + j)) {
					var trObj = document.getElementById(trId + j);
					
					var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
					for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
						elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}




function trHighlightJoin(trId) {

	var trObj = document.getElementById(trId);
	if (trObj.tagName == "A") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement.parentElement;
	} else if (trObj.tagName == "TD") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement;
	}

	var parentcolor = window.getComputedStyle(trObj.lastElementChild).backgroundColor;
	parentcolor = rgbtohex(parentcolor);

	var highlightcolor = "#" + darken(parentcolor, -32);

	var beforecolor = "";
	var colorVal = "";
	
	var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
	
	for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
		if (elemets[i].rowSpan == 1) {
				beforecolor = elemets[i].style.backgroundColor;
				break;
		}
	}
	
	if (beforecolor == "" || beforecolor == "#ffffff") {
		colorVal = highlightcolor;
	} else {
		colorVal = ""
	}
	
	
	for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
		if (elemets[i].rowSpan == 1) {
				elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
		}
	}
}
	




function darken(hexstr, scalefactor) {
	var maxColor = parseInt('ff', 16);
	var minColor = parseInt('00', 16);
	var r = scalefactor;
	var a, i;
	
	
	if ((typeof(hexstr) != 'string') || (typeof(scalefactor) != 'number')) {
		return hexstr;
	}

	
	hexstr = hexstr.replace(/[^0-9a-f]+/ig, '');

	
	if (hexstr.length == 3) {
		a = hexstr.split('');
	} else if (hexstr.length == 6) {
		a = hexstr.match(/(\w{2})/g);
	} else {
		return hexstr;
	}

	
	for (i=0; i<a.length; i++) {
		if (a[i].length == 2) {
			a[i] = parseInt(a[i], 16);
		} else {
			a[i] = parseInt(a[i], 16);
			a[i] = a[i]*16 + a[i];
		}
	}

	function _darken(a) {
		
		var ret =  a + r;
		
		if (ret > maxColor) {
			ret = maxColor;
		}
		if (ret < minColor) {
			ret = minColor;
		}
		return ret;
	}

	
	for (i=0; i<a.length; i++) {
		a[i] = _darken(a[i]);
		a[i] = Math.floor(a[i]).toString(16);
		if (a[i].length == 1) {
			a[i] = '0' + a[i];
		}
	}
	return a.join('');
}




var visitedTrColor = "#ffd720"; 
var visitedTrHighlightColor = "#"+darken(visitedTrColor, -32); 
function trBgColorChangeVisited(trId, feedback, cons, colorVal) {

	if (!colorVal) {
		colorVal = visitedTrColor;
	}

	var trObj = document.getElementById(trId);
	if (trObj.tagName == "A") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement.parentElement;
	} else if (trObj.tagName == "TD") {
		trObj = document.getElementById(trId).parentElement;
	}
	
	var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");

	for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
		if ((feedback) && ((i<2) || (i>elemets.length - 3))) {
		} else {
			if (elemets[i].style.backgroundColor == visitedTrHighlightColor) {
				
				return;
			}
			elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
		}
	}

	
	elemets = trObj.nextElementSibling.getElementsByTagName("td");
	
	for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
		if ((feedback) && ((i<2) || (i>elemets.length - 3))) {
		} else {
			elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
		}
	}

	
	if (cons) {
		if (document.getElementById(trId + "1")) {
			var trObj = document.getElementById(trId + "1");
			
			var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
			for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
				elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
			}
		}
	} else {
		if (trObj.firstChild.rowSpan) {
			var trRowSpan = trObj.firstChild.rowSpan;
			for (j = 1; j < trRowSpan; j++) {
				if (document.getElementById(trId + j)) {
					var trObj = document.getElementById(trId + j);
					
					var elemets = trObj.getElementsByTagName("td");
					for (i = 0; i < elemets.length; i++) {
						elemets[i].style.backgroundColor = colorVal;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}



//rgbからhexに変換
function rgbtohex(orig){
	var rgb = orig.replace(/\s/g,'').match(/^rgba?\((\d+),(\d+),(\d+)/i);
	return (rgb && rgb.length === 4) ? "#" +
	 ("0" + parseInt(rgb[1],10).toString(16)).slice(-2) +
	 ("0" + parseInt(rgb[2],10).toString(16)).slice(-2) +
	 ("0" + parseInt(rgb[3],10).toString(16)).slice(-2) : orig;
}

function fileFieldChengeDisabled(fieldId, checkboxId, checkBoxDisabled) {

	// すでにサーバー処理で無効にされている場合は有効にしない
	if (document.getElementById(fieldId) && document.getElementById(checkboxId)
		&& !document.getElementById(checkboxId).disabled) {
		document.getElementById(fieldId).disabled = document.getElementById(checkboxId).checked;
		document.getElementById(checkboxId).disabled = checkBoxDisabled;
	}

}

function setReadOnly(obj) {

	obj.readOnly = true;
	if (obj.className == "") {
		obj.className = "TEXT_READONLY"
	} else {
		obj.className += " TEXT_READONLY"
	}
}

}

function checkAlertCount() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var isCheckAlert = '0';
	if (isCheckAlert == '1') {
		


		var ret = confirm('YQ10071W: It exceeds the alert figure of superseding. Do you continue to transact it?');


		if (ret) {
			
			document.syqaa090Form.isExecCheckAlertCount.value = '0';
			document.getElementById("syqaa090Form_buttonControltjKanryo").click();
		} else {
			return;
		}
	}
}


function showSearch() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var retVal = new Array();
	var encodeType = null;
	var paramAry = {};

	param1  = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value;

	if (param1 == MST_KBN_COUNTRY) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "03";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMPORNENT) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "04";
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductCategory")[0].value;
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.tyq0700productGCodeVal1")[0].value;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_SUPPLIER) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "05";
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseView").innerText;
		

		paramAry["param4"] = "1"
		
		

		paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode"] =
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode")[0].value;


		var partsSupplyTypeList = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType");
		for (var i = 0 ; i < partsSupplyTypeList.length ; i++) {

			// 部品区分が非活性の場合（該当する属性名のオブジェクトが生成されていない）
			// ※選択されているとサイズは1, 選択されていないとサイズは0となる
			if (partsSupplyTypeList.length < 2) {
				paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType"] = partsSupplyTypeList[i].value;
				break;
			// 部品区分が活性の場合
			} else {
				// 該当する属性名オブジェクトが2つ生成されているため、チェックされた方のみ値を取得する
				if (partsSupplyTypeList[i].checked) {
					paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType"] = partsSupplyTypeList[i].value;
					break;
				}
			}
			
		}
		paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyBaseCode"] =
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyBaseCode")[0].value;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_FACTORY) {
	
		paramAry["param1"] = "10";

	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_TROUBLE_COMPLAINT) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "06";
		paramAry["param2"] = "1";
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementById("L00791").innerText;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_TROUBLE_DEFECT) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "06";
		paramAry["param2"] = "2";
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementById("L00792").innerText;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_TROUBLE_DESTRINATION) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "26";
		
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode")[0].value.trim();
		
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryName")[0].value;

		
		if (paramAry["param2"] == "") {
			
			var param = 'Production Base';
			alert('YQ00015E: Select ' + param + '!');
			return;
		}
		
		encodeType = "2";
		
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_TROUBLE_MANUFACTURE_FACTORY) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "10";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_TROUBLE_ENGINE_MODEL) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "14";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_ENGINE_SPEC) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "16";
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoEngineCharger")[0].value;
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoVvtFlg")[0].value;
		paramAry["param4"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoLeanBurnFlg")[0].value;
		paramAry["param5"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoHybridFlg")[0].value;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_MISSION_MODEL) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "17";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_NOT_REASON) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "08";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_PET_NAME) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "21";
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductCategory")[0].value;
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.tyq0700productGCodeVal1")[0].value;
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMPONENT_NAME) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "22";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMPONENT_SEGMENTATION_CODE) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "23";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMMENT_INPUT) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "24";
	}

	
	if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMPORNENT) {
		
		var width = '900';
		var heigth = '600';
	} else {
		var width = '400';
		var heigth = '600';
	}

	
	//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E05SFnd',paramAry,'',width,heigth, encodeType);
	comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E05SFnd',paramAry,'showSearch',width,heigth, encodeType);
	
	return false;
	

}



function showSearchReturn(){
	var retVal;
	var param1 = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	

	
	for(var i = 0; i < retVal.length; i++) {
		if(retVal[i]) {
			retVal[i] = retVal[i].replace("&#44;", ",");
		}
	}

	
	
	if (typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		return;
	}


	
	updateCheckInputChange("true");


	if (param1 == MST_KBN_COUNTRY) {

		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1OutbreakCountryCode")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CountryName")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1OutbreakCountryCodeView").innerText = " ";

			
			dispSeriousIssueFlg();
			return;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1OutbreakCountryCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CountryName")[0].value = retVal[2];
		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1OutbreakCountryCodeView").innerText = retVal[2];

		
		dispSeriousIssueFlg();

	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_COMPORNENT) {

		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ComponentCode")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ComponentName")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_f1ComponentCodeView").innerText = " ";
			return;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ComponentCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ComponentName")[0].value = retVal[1] + " : " + retVal[2];
		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_f1ComponentCodeView").innerText = retVal[1] + " : " + retVal[2];
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_SUPPLIER) {

		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SupplierCdOfCausalParts")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SupplierCdOfCausalPartsName")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SupplierCdOfCausalPartsView").innerText = " ";

			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SupplierBaseCode")[0].value = " ";

			return;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SupplierCdOfCausalParts")[0].value = retVal[1];

		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SupplierCdOfCausalPartsName")[0].value = retVal[6];
		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SupplierCdOfCausalPartsView").innerText = retVal[6];

function showSearch3(i, j) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var retVal = new Array();
	var paramAry = {};

	param1  = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value;

	if (param1 == MST_KBN_NOT_REASON) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "08";
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_SUPPLIER) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "05";
		
		paramAry["param2"] = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].g1CauseAnalysisBaseCode')[0].value; 
		paramAry["param3"] = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].causeAnalysisBaseName')[0].value; 

		
		paramAry["param4"] = "1"
	

		paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode"] =
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode")[0].value;


		var partsSupplyTypeList = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType");

		for (var k = 0 ; k < partsSupplyTypeList.length ; k++) {
			// 部品区分が非活性の場合（該当する属性名のオブジェクトが生成されていない）
			// ※選択されているとサイズは1, 選択されていないとサイズは0となる
			if (partsSupplyTypeList.length < 2) {
				paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType"] = partsSupplyTypeList[k].value;
				break;
			// 部品区分が活性の場合
			} else {
				// 該当する属性名オブジェクトが2つ生成されているため、チェックされた方のみ値を取得する
				if (partsSupplyTypeList[k].checked) {
					paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyType"] = partsSupplyTypeList[k].value;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		paramAry["syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyBaseCode"] =
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1PartsSupplyBaseCode")[0].value;




	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_MEETING_MEMO_NAME) {
		
		paramAry["param1"] = "32";

	}

	
	var width = '400';
	var heigth = '600';


	
	var returnAry = [];
	returnAry.push(i, j, param1);
	
	document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();

	
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = "";
	document.getElementsByName("param2")[0].value = "";
	
	//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E05SFnd',paramAry,'',width,heigth);
	comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E05SFnd',paramAry,'showSearch3',width,heigth);
	
	return false;
	

}



function showSearch3Return(){
	var retVal;
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
  	
	
	var i = returnAry[0];
	var j = returnAry[1];
	var param1 = returnAry[2];
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	

	
	for(var i2 = 0; i2 < retVal.length; i2++) {
		if(retVal[i2]) {
			retVal[i2] = retVal[i2].replace("&#44;", ",");
		}
	}

	
	
	if (typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		return;
	}


	
	updateCheckInputChange("true");


	if (param1 == MST_KBN_NOT_REASON) {
		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CountermeasureReasonCode")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CountermeasureReason")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementById("syqaa090NotMeasuresReasonViewEntity[" + i + "]_g1NotMeasuresReasonView").innerText = " ";
			return;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CountermeasureReasonCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
		document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CountermeasureReason")[0].value = retVal[2];
		document.getElementById("syqaa090NotMeasuresReasonViewEntity[" + i + "]_g1NotMeasuresReasonView").innerText = retVal[2];
	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_SUPPLIER) {
		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleSpplCd" + j)[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleSpplName" + j)[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleSpplView" + i + "_" + j).innerText = " ";

			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleBaseCd" + j)[0].value = " ";

			return;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleSpplCd" + j)[0].value = retVal[1];

		document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleSpplName" + j)[0].value = retVal[6];
		document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleSpplView" + i + "_" + j).innerText = retVal[6];
		document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseResponsibleBaseCd" + j)[0].value = retVal[5];


	} else if (param1 == MST_KBN_MEETING_MEMO_NAME) {
		
		if (retVal[0] == "0") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceCode")[0].value = " ";
			document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceName")[0].value = " ";
			return;
		}
		
		document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
		document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceName")[0].value = retVal[2];

	}
}




function saveButtonInfo(uniqueNumber, proccessCode, chargePersonCode, roleCategory, 
														proccessKbn, notExecApproval, rowNo) {

	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "documentId")[0].value = 'IN202310B06811';
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "uniqueNumber")[0].value = uniqueNumber;
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "proccessCode")[0].value = proccessCode;
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "chargePersonCode")[0].value = chargePersonCode;
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "roleCategory")[0].value = roleCategory;
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "proccessKbn")[0].value = proccessKbn;

	if ('true' == notExecApproval) {
		notExecApproval = true;
	} else if ('false' == notExecApproval) {
		notExecApproval = false;
	}

	if (notExecApproval) {
		notExecApproval = '1';
	} else if (!notExecApproval) {
		notExecApproval = '0';
	}

	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "notExecApproval")[0].value = notExecApproval;
	document.getElementsByName(beanPath + "rowNo")[0].value = rowNo;

	return;
}



function saveButtonInfoPopUp(param1Val) {
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = param1Val;
}



function saveOperationRoleExtension(val) {
	document.getElementsByName("operationRoleExtension")[0].value = val;
}



function initDispTreatmentJudgmentFlg() {




	dispTreatmentJudgmentFlg(0);

}




function dispTreatmentJudgmentFlg(index) {

	impossibleToAscertainFlg = document.getElementById("impossibleToAscertainFlg_" + index).checked;
	noResponsibilityFlg = document.getElementById("noResponsibilityFlg_" + index).checked;
	substantialFlg = document.getElementById("substantialFlg_" + index).checked;
	noResponsibilityBaseFlg = document.getElementById("noResponsibilityBaseFlg_" + index).checked;
	
	treatmentJudgmentCheckEnd = document.getElementById("treatmentJudgmentCheckEnd_" + index);
	treatmentJudgmentCheckCont = document.getElementById("treatmentJudgmentCheckCont_" + index);

	if (impossibleToAscertainFlg || noResponsibilityFlg || substantialFlg || noResponsibilityBaseFlg) {
		treatmentJudgmentCheckEnd.style.display = "";
		treatmentJudgmentCheckCont.style.display = "none";

		document.getElementById("g1ReproducibilityRequiredMark_" + index).style.display = "none";

	} else {
		treatmentJudgmentCheckEnd.style.display = "none";
		treatmentJudgmentCheckCont.style.display = "";

		document.getElementById("g1ReproducibilityRequiredMark_" + index).style.display = "inline";

	}
}


function setSeriousIssueFlg() {
	

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}



	var issueFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SeriousIssueFlg")[0];		
	var issueFlgString = $("ISSUEFLG");					
	var issueFlgStringAF = $("ISSUEFLG_ACCIDENT_FIRE");	

	var primaryFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1RankPrimaryJudgement")[0].value;			
	var secondaryFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1RankSecondaryJudgement")[0].value;		

	
	var mlitCodeFirstS = document.getElementById("MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS");			
	var mlitCodeFirstA = document.getElementById("MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT");			
	var mlitCodeSecondS = document.getElementById("MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS");			
	var mlitCodeSecondA = document.getElementById("MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT");			
	var mlitCodeMainS = document.getElementById("MLIT_FORM1_INVESTIGATE_REPORT");			
	var mlitCodeMainA = document.getElementById("MLIT_FORM2_INVESTIGATE_REPORT");			


	
	if (arguments.length > 0
		&& 14 == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value
		&& false == true) {
		var apCompleteDate = document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;
		var infoCompleteDate = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

		if (arguments[0] == 1) {
			
			
			if (primaryFlg == 'A') {
				if (document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled) {
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled = false;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitFirstJudgmentCode");
					}
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitFirstJudgmentCode");
					}
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitFirstJudgmentCode");
					}
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF'));
					document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ItemControlViewEntity.disabledFlgMlitFirst")[0].value = false;
				}
			} else {
				if (apCompleteDate != "" && !document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled) {
					clearRadio('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitFirstJudgmentCode');
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF').disabled = true;
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_FIRST_JUDGMENT_OFF'));
					document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ItemControlViewEntity.disabledFlgMlitFirst")[0].value = true;
				}
			}
		} else if (arguments[0] == 2) {
			
			
			if (secondaryFlg == 'A') {
				if (document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled) {
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled = false;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitSecondJudgmentCode");
					}
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitSecondJudgmentCode");
					}
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF').disabled = false;
					if (!document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF').getAttribute("name")) {
						document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF').setAttribute("name","syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitSecondJudgmentCode");
					}
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF'));
					document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ItemControlViewEntity.disabledFlgMlitSecond")[0].value = false;
				}
			} else {
				if (infoCompleteDate != "" && !document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled) {
					clearRadio('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1MlitSecondJudgmentCode');
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_CLEAR_BUTTON').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT').disabled = true;
					document.getElementById('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF').disabled = true;
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_SERIOUS'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_ACCIDENT'));
					siftCtrl.changeDisable($('MLIT_SECOND_JUDGMENT_OFF'));
					document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ItemControlViewEntity.disabledFlgMlitSecond")[0].value = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}


	
	if (mlitCodeFirstS.checked || mlitCodeFirstA.checked || mlitCodeSecondS.checked
			|| mlitCodeSecondA.checked || mlitCodeMainS.checked || mlitCodeMainA.checked) {
		
		issueFlg.value = '1';
		issueFlgString.style.display = "inline";
	} else {
		if (issueFlg.value == '1') {
			
			alert('YQ10126I: As the selection is changed, this is excluded from report items to MLIT as serious issue.');
		}
		issueFlg.value = '0';
		issueFlgString.style.display = "none";
	}
	
	
	if (mlitCodeFirstA.checked || mlitCodeSecondA.checked || mlitCodeMainA.checked) {
		issueFlgStringAF.style.display = "inline";
	} else {
		issueFlgStringAF.style.display = "none";
	}


	if (arguments[0] == 1 || arguments[0] == 2) {
		if (primaryFlg != "" || secondaryFlg != "") {
			
				
				defaultPendingDate(0);
			
		}
	}


}


function setSeriousIssueFlgRK() {
	
	
	var issueFlgStringRK = $("ISSUEFLG_RK");			
	
	
	var mlitCodeFirst = $("MLIT_RK_RELAPSE_FLG");				
	var mlitCodeMain = $("MLIT_FORM4_INVESTIGATE_REPORT");	
	
	
	if (mlitCodeFirst.checked || mlitCodeMain.checked) {
		issueFlgStringRK.style.display = "inline";
	} else {
		issueFlgStringRK.style.display = "none";
	}

	
	var mlitRKRelapseFlg = $("MLIT_RK_RELAPSE_FLG");
	if (mlitRKRelapseFlg.checked && MLIT.style.display == "none") {
		var flgName = "MLIT";
		document.getElementById(flgName).style.display = "";
		dispFoldControlFolderImage(flgName, true);
	}

}

function dispSeriousIssueFlg() {

	
	var country = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1OutbreakCountryCode")[0].value;		
	var baseCode = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;		

	var mlitAreaLink = document.getElementById("MLIT_Area_Link");		
	var mlitAreaFirst = document.getElementById("MLIT_Area_First");		
	var mlitAreaSecond = document.getElementById("MLIT_Area_Second");	
	var mlitAreaMain = document.getElementById("MLIT_Area_Main");		
	var issueFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SeriousIssueFlg")[0];		
	var issueFlgString = $("ISSUEFLG");					
	var issueFlgStringAF = $("ISSUEFLG_ACCIDENT_FIRE");	
	var issueFlgStringRK = $("ISSUEFLG_RK");			
	
	var showAreaDisplay = 'none';				
	
	
	if (country == "100" 
		&& baseCode == "01") {
		showAreaDisplay = '';
	}
	mlitAreaLink.style.display = showAreaDisplay;
	mlitAreaFirst.style.display = showAreaDisplay;
	mlitAreaSecond.style.display = showAreaDisplay;
	mlitAreaMain.style.display = showAreaDisplay;
	
	if (showAreaDisplay == '') {
		
		setSeriousIssueFlg();
		setSeriousIssueFlgRK();
	} else {
		
		issueFlg.value = '0';
		issueFlgString.style.display = "none";
		issueFlgStringAF.style.display = "none";
		issueFlgStringRK.style.display = "none";
	}
}


function dispControl(id) {

	var element = document.getElementById(id);

	if (element.style.display == "none") {
		element.style.display = "";
	} else {
		element.style.display = "none";
	}
}


function dispControl2(id, isVisible) {
	var element = document.getElementById(id);

	if (isVisible) {
		element.style.display = "";
	} else {
		element.style.display = "none";
	}
}

function dispFoldControl(id,name,isProcessNameLink) {


	var element = document.getElementById(id);

	if (element.style.display == "none") {

		if (!isProcessNameLink) {
			element.style.display = "";
		}

		
		document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value = "open";
		dispFoldControlFolderImage(name, true);
	} else {

		if (!isProcessNameLink) {
			element.style.display = "none";
		}

		
		document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value = "close";
		dispFoldControlFolderImage(name, false);
	}
	
	
	document.body.style.display = 'none';
	document.body.style.display = '';
}


function initDispFoldControl(id,name) {
	document.getElementById(id).style.display = document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value;
}

function dispFoldControld(name) {
	if (document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value == "none") {
		
		document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value = "block";
	} else {
		
		document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value = "none";
	}
}


function dispFoldControlFolderImage(id,displayFlg) {

	var obj = document.getElementsByName(id + "Image");
	
	if (obj) {
		
		for (var i = 0; i < obj.length; i++) {
			if (displayFlg) {
				obj[i].src = "/sift/icons/omit-page-orange.gif";
				obj[i].alt = "Foldable";
			} else {
				obj[i].src = "/sift/icons/add-page-orange.gif";
				obj[i].alt = "Developable";
			}
		}
	}
}


function clearRadio(name) {
	var i;
	var element = document.getElementsByName(name);

	for (i=0; i<element.length; i++) {
		element[i].checked = false;
	}
	
	if (name == "syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ActionJudgementCode") {
		
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession")[0].value = "";
		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SbIdSupersessionDisplay").innerText = "";

		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_manufactureFactorySelect").disabled = false;

<!-- @UPD 2015.04.23 START mizoguchi #1575 生産情報を再読み込み（リフレッシュ）させる機能を追加する -->
		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_vehicleInformationSearch").disabled = false;
<!-- @UPD 2015.04.23 END   mizoguchi #1575 生産情報を再読み込み（リフレッシュ）させる機能を追加する -->

		document.getElementById("syqaa090_sbIdRank").style.display = "none";
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090Form_g1RankSecondaryJudgement").value = "";
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090Form_g1SupersedeFtirs").value = "";

	}


	
	updateCheckInputChange("true");

}

		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SbIdSupersessionDisplay").innerText = "";

		document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_manufactureFactorySelect").disabled = false;

		document.getElementById("SYQAA090Form_g1SupersedeFtirs").value = "";

	}
}


function changeRadio(value) {

	var element1 = document.getElementById("HOSOKULABEL");	
	var element2 = document.getElementById("RIYUABEL");		
	var element3 = document.getElementById("CHECKAPPROVELink"); 
	var element4 = document.getElementById('CHECKAPPROVE');
	var element5 = $('trNonacceptanceReasonCode');				
	var element6 = document.getElementById("CHECKAPPROVELinkSpan"); 
	
	if (value == "1") {
		
		if (element1) {
			element1.style.display = "";
		}
		if (element2) {
			element2.style.display = "none";
		}
		if (element3) {
			element3.disabled = true;
			element3.removeAttribute("href");
			if (element4 && (element4.style.display == "")) {
				comChangeDisp('CHECKAPPROVE');
			}
		}
		if (element5) {
			element5.style.display = "none";
		}
	} else if (value == "2") {
		
		if (element1) {
			element1.style.display = "none";
		}
		if (element2) {
			element2.style.display = "";
		}
		if (element3) {


			element3.disabled = false;
			element3.href = "#";

		}
		if (element5) {
			element5.style.display = "";
		}
	} else if (value == "3") {
		
		if (element1) {
			element1.style.display = "";
		}
		if (element2) {
			element2.style.display = "none";
		}
		if (element3) {
			element3.disabled = true;
			element3.removeAttribute("href");
			if (element4 && (element4.style.display == "")) {
				comChangeDisp('CHECKAPPROVE');
			}
		}
		if (element5) {
			element5.style.display = "none";
		}
	} else {
		
		if (element1) {
			element1.style.display = "";
		}
		if (element2) {
			element2.style.display = "none";
		}
		if (element3) {
			element3.disabled = true;
			element3.removeAttribute("href");
			if (element4 && (element4.style.display == "")) {
				comChangeDisp('CHECKAPPROVE');
			}
		}
		if (element5) {
			element5.style.display = "none";
		}
	}
	
	
	element6.disabled = element3.disabled;
}

function changeRadioFeedback(value, isInitializing) {

	
	document.getElementById('feedbackInformationViewEntity_g1FeedbackReplyWayCategory').value = value;

	if (false) {
		if (value == "3") {
			document.getElementById('feedbackCompleteButton').disabled = false;
		} else {
			document.getElementById('feedbackCompleteButton').disabled = true;
		}
	}
	
	

	if (value == "1" || value.trim() == '') {// "代表回答をそのまま使用" "クリアボタン"

		
		$("feedbackFile").disabled = true;
		$("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.feedbackAttachmentFileBtn").disabled = true;
		$("feedbackFileCheckbox").disabled = true;
		if(! isInitializing){
			$("feedbackFileCheckbox").checked = false;
		}
	} else { // "代表回答を修正して使用" "個別に回答"
		$("feedbackFile").disabled = 
			($("feedbackFile").style.defaultDisabled == "true");
		$("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.feedbackAttachmentFileBtn").disabled = 
			($("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.feedbackAttachmentFileBtn").style.defaultDisabled == "true");
		$("feedbackFileCheckbox").disabled = 
			($("feedbackFile").style.defaultDisabled == "true");

	}
}


function changeCountermeasureNeedRadio(index, value) {

	var element10 = document.getElementById("g1CountermeasureNeedFlg_FLG_ON_INPUT_0" + index);
	var element11 = document.getElementById("g1CountermeasureNeedFlg_FLG_ON_INPUT_1" + index);
	var element2 = document.getElementById("g1CountermeasureNeedFlg_FLG_OFF_INPUT_" + index);

	var seppenTable = $("SEPPEN_TABLE_" + index);

	if (element10 && element11 && element2) {
		if (value == "1") {
			element10.style.display = "";
			element11.style.display = "";
			element2.style.display = "none";
			seppenTable.style.display = "";
		} else if (value == "0") {
			element10.style.display = "none";
			element11.style.display = "none";
			element2.style.display = "";
			seppenTable.style.display = "none";
		} else if (value == "99") {
			element10.style.display = "none";
			element11.style.display = "none";
			element2.style.display = "none";
			document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + index + "].g1CountermeasureNeedFlg")[2].value = "";
			seppenTable.style.display = "";
		}
	}
}


function sendMail(adr, sub, bdy) {

	if (sub == '') {
		var str = "mailto:" + adr + "?body=" + bdy;
	} else {
		var str = "mailto:" + adr + "?subject=" + sub + "&body=" + bdy;
	}

	window.location.href = str;
}


function sendFtirMail() {
<!-- @UPD 2020.03.20 START sugiura -->
	var adr = document.getElementById("reporterMailaddr").value;
<!-- @UPD 2020.03.20 END sugiura -->
	var mailBody;
	
	
	mailBody = "Sales Model Code: " + leftPlus(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SalesModelCode")[0].value.trim(),50) + "\n";
	if ("" == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FaultSubject")[0].value.trim()) {
		mailBody += "Subject: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FaultSubjectLl")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
	} else {
		mailBody += "Subject: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FaultSubject")[0].value.trim() + "\n";

		if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FaultSubjectLl")[0].value.trim()) {
			mailBody += document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FaultSubjectLl")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
		}

	}

	mailBody += "Causal Parts No.: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseNo")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
	if ("" == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseName")[0].value.trim()) {
		mailBody += "Causal Parts Name: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseNameLl")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	} else {
		mailBody += "Causal Parts Name: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseName")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
		if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseNameLl")[0].value.trim()) {
			mailBody += document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1MainCauseNameLl")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
		}
		mailBody += "\n";
	}

	
	
	mailBody += "Date of Incident: ";
	if ("" == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1FailureDate")[0].value.trim()) {
		mailBody += "\n";
	} else {
		mailBody += "25.Oct.2023\n";
	}
	mailBody += "Date Reported: ";
	if ("" == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1ReportDate")[0].value.trim()) {
		mailBody += "\n\n";
	} else {
		mailBody += "25.Oct.2023\n\n";
	}
	
	
	mailBody += "Report Company: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1ReportCompany")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
	mailBody += "Person Approved: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1Reporter")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	
	
	mailBody += "Issued Company: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1WriteCompany")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
	mailBody += "Person Issued: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1Writer")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	
	
	mailBody += "Issue No.: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1IssueNo")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
	mailBody += "FTIR No.: " + document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	
	sendMail(adr, '', EscapeSJIS(mailBody));
}


function execAjaxMail() {

	if (document.getElementsByName(arguments[2])[0]) {
		var userId = document.getElementsByName(arguments[2])[0].value;
	} else {
		var userId = document.getElementById(arguments[2]).value;
	}


	
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = arguments[0];
	document.getElementsByName("param2")[0].value = arguments[1];
	
	
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: true,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,
		parameters: "field=G_EMAIL_ADDRESS&key=" + userId, 
		onComplete: callBackAjaxMail
	}
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/userInfoSearch.do", ajaxOptions);
}


function execAjaxMailWishList() {
	var userId = "";
	


	
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = arguments[0];
	document.getElementsByName("param2")[0].value = arguments[1];
	
	
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: true,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,
		parameters: "field=G_EMAIL_ADDRESS&delimiter=;&key=" + userId, 
		onComplete: callBackAjaxMail
	}
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/userInfoSearch.do", ajaxOptions);
}


function callBackAjaxMail(request) {
	var address;
	var url;

	var mailSubjectEn;
	var mailSubjectLl;
	var mailSubject;
	var mailSubjectLng;
	var mailSubjectLenMax;

	var mailBody;
	var ftirId = document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value.trim();
	var process = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value.trim();
	var processEx = document.getElementsByName("param2")[0].value.trim();

	if (request == null) {
		address = "";
	} else {
		address = request.responseText;
	}
	

	
	mailSubject = "[SIFT]" + ftirId;
	
	mailSubjectEn = " " + "Subject:" + " " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0].value.trim();
	
	mailSubjectLl = " " + "Subject:" + " " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim();
	if ('en_US' == 'en_US') {
		
		if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0].value.trim()) {
			
			mailSubject += mailSubjectEn;
			mailSubjectLng = 'en_US';
		} else {
			if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim()) {
				
				mailSubject += mailSubjectLl;
				mailSubjectLng = '';
			}
		}
	} else {
	
		if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim()) {
			
			mailSubject += mailSubjectLl;
			mailSubjectLng = '';
		} else {
			if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0].value.trim()) {
				
				mailSubject += mailSubjectEn;
				mailSubjectLng = 'en_US';
			}
		}
	}
	if ('en_US' == mailSubjectLng) {
		
		mailSubjectLenMax = 340;
	} else {
		
		mailSubjectLenMax = 170;
	}
	
	if (mailSubject.length > mailSubjectLenMax) {
		mailSubject = mailSubject.substr(0, mailSubjectLenMax); 
	}

	

	url = "SYQAA710E04SFnd.do?action=/SYQAA090E00SFnd&g1FtirId=" + ftirId + 
		"&g1ProccessCode=" + process + "&g1ProccessCodeExtension=" + processEx;

		
	mailBody = "Product Model Code: " + leftPlus(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductModelCode")[0].value.trim(),50) + "\n";
	mailBody += "Sales Model Code: " + leftPlus(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SalesModelCode")[0].value.trim(),50) + "\n\n";
	if ("" == document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0].value.trim()) {
		mailBody += "Subject: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	} else {
		mailBody += "Subject: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0].value.trim() + "\n";

		if ("" != document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim()) {
			mailBody += document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0].value.trim() + "\n";
		}
		mailBody += "\n";

	}
	mailBody += "Causal Parts No.: " + document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CausalPartsNo")[0].value.trim() + "\n\n";
	mailBody += "FTIR No.: " + ftirId + "\n";



	mailBody += "https://sift.bizapps.suzuki/sift/" + url + "\n";

	



	

	sendMail(address, EscapeSJIS(mailSubject), EscapeSJIS(mailBody));

}

function leftPlus(str, len) {
	if (str.length <= len) {
		return str;
	} else {
		return str.substring(0, len) + " ...";
	}
}


function copyAnswer() {

	var source = document.getElementsByName("feedbackReplyWayCategoryNoCorrection")[0];
	var destination = document.getElementsByName("feedbackReplyWayCategoryCorrection")[0];

	destination.value = source.value;
	if (document.getElementById("feedbackType") != null) {
		document.getElementById("feedbackType").style.visibility = "visible";
	}
}


function initCheckBox(name) {

	var checkBoxValue = document.getElementsByName(name)[0].value;

	if (checkBoxValue == "1") {
		document.getElementById(name + "CheckBox").value = "1";
	}
}


function openDiscussionHistory(conductionBaseCode) {

	
	var ftirNo = document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value;
	
	var g1ChargePersonCode = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
	
	var paramAry = {};

	paramAry["param1"] = ftirNo;
	paramAry["param2"] = conductionBaseCode;
	paramAry["param3"] = g1ChargePersonCode;

	
	var width = '1008';
	var heigth = '-1';

	comOpenWindow('/SYQAA090E52SFnd','SYQAA070' + ftirNo + conductionBaseCode,paramAry,width,heigth);
}
