				document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = retVal[6];
				document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = retVal[7];
				document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
				document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = retVal[4];
			}

		}
	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_FTIR_CAUSE_ANALYSIS) {
		position = param1.substring(1);
		
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptCode" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptName" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptNameDisplay" + position).innerText = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptCode" + position).value = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptName" + position).value = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("causeAnalysisViewEntity_g1CauseResponsibleDeptNameDisplay" + position).innerText = retVal[4];
		}
	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_REPORT) {
		position = param1.substring(1);
		

		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonNameDisplay_" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonNameDisplay_" + position).innerText = "";
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonCode_" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonName_" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("hidChargeDeptCode_" + position).value = "";
			document.getElementById("hidChargeDeptAbbreviation_" + position).value = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {


			
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonNameDisplay_" + position).value = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("chargePersonNameDisplay_" + position).innerText = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonCode_" + position).value = retVal[6];
			document.getElementById("hidChargePersonName_" + position).value = retVal[7];
			document.getElementById("hidChargeDeptCode_" + position).value = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("hidChargeDeptAbbreviation_" + position).value = retVal[4];
		}


	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_MYTASK) {
		position = param1.substring(1);
		
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList_mytaskChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + position + "]").innerText = "";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			
			if (processChargePersonInfo) {
				
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonNameDisplay;
				document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList_mytaskChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + position + "]").innerText = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonNameDisplay;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonCode;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonName;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargeDeptCode;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargeDeptAbbreviation;
			} else {
				
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = dispInfomation;
				document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList_mytaskChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + position + "]").innerText = dispInfomation;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = retVal[6];
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = retVal[7];
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + position + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = retVal[4];
			}
		}


	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_TSR) {
		
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementById("tsrChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay").innerText = "";
			document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = "";
			document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {

			
			if (processChargePersonInfo) {
				
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonNameDisplay;
				document.getElementById("tsrChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay").innerText = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonNameDisplay;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonCode;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargePersonName;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargeDeptCode;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = processChargePersonInfo.chargeDeptAbbreviation;
			} else {
				
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value = dispInfomation;
				document.getElementById("tsrChargePersonViewEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay").innerText = dispInfomation;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = retVal[6];
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonName")[0].value = retVal[7];
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value = retVal[1];
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value = retVal[4];
			}
		}


	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_DESIGN_CHARGE_DEPT) {
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			document.getElementById('hid_designChargeDeptCode').value = "";
			document.getElementById('span_designChargeDeptNamePl').innerText = "";
			document.getElementById('hid_designChargeDeptNamePl').value = "";
			document.getElementById('hid_designChargeDeptNameEn').value = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
			
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			document.getElementById("hid_designChargeDeptCode").value = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("span_designChargeDeptNamePl").innerText = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hid_designChargeDeptNamePl").value = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hid_designChargeDeptNameEn").value = retVal[5];
			
		}
	} else if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_QUALITY_CHARGE_DEPT) {
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			document.getElementById('hid_qualityChargeDeptCode').value = "";
			document.getElementById('span_qualityChargeDeptNamePl').innerText = "";
			document.getElementById('hid_qualityChargeDeptNamePl').value = "";
			document.getElementById('hid_qualityChargeDeptNameEn').value = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value = "";
			return false;
			
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			document.getElementById("hid_qualityChargeDeptCode").value = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("span_qualityChargeDeptNamePl").innerText = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hid_qualityChargeDeptNamePl").value = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hid_qualityChargeDeptNameEn").value = retVal[5];
		}
	}
	
	// TODO
	var assignActionPath = document.getElementById('assignActionPath').value;
	
	if (assignActionPath != "") {
		document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + assignActionPath + ".do";
		document.forms[0].submit();
	}
	

	return true;

}




function openSubAssginWindow(prcsKbn, index, deptOrUserKbn, dispKbnKouho, dispKbnTree, dispKbnProcess, dispKbnUnselect, 
							authorityControlKbn, blockKbn) {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}

	var retVal = new Array();
	var paramAry = {};
	var position;
	var dispInfomation;

	param1  = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value;

	
	paramAry["prcsKbn"]             = prcsKbn;								// 処理プロセス区分
	paramAry["deptOrUserKbn"]       = deptOrUserKbn;						// 部署/担当者区分
	paramAry["dispKbnKouho"]        = dispKbnKouho;							// 表示区分1(候補)
	paramAry["dispKbnTree"]         = dispKbnTree;							// 表示区分2(ツリー)
	paramAry["dispKbnProcess"]      = dispKbnProcess;						// 表示区分3(プロセス)
	paramAry["dispKbnUnselect"]     = dispKbnUnselect;						// 未選択表示区分
	paramAry["g1DispUserCode"]      = getProcessChargeDeptCode(param1);
	paramAry["authorityControlKbn"] = authorityControlKbn;
	paramAry["g1FtirId"]            = 'IN202310B06811';			// 文書番号


	if (param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_FTIR_CAUSE_ANALYSIS
		|| param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_DESIGN_CHARGE_DEPT
		|| param1.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_QUALITY_CHARGE_DEPT) {
		paramAry["systemOpenFlg"] = '0';

	} else {
		paramAry["systemOpenFlg"] = '1';
	}
	paramAry["g1RoleCategory"] = 'S';   // 役割区分

	paramAry["useUserSerachFlg"] = '1';


	
	var returnAry = [];
	returnAry.push(prcsKbn, index, deptOrUserKbn, blockKbn);
	
	document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();
	
	
	var width = '600';
	var heigth = '550';

	
	//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E28SFnd',paramAry,'',width,heigth);
	if (deptOrUserKbn == '0'){
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E28SFnd',paramAry,'openSubAssginWindow0',width,heigth);
	} else {
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090E28SFnd',paramAry,'openSubAssginWindow',width,heigth);
	}
	
	return false;
	

}



function openSubAssginWindowReturn(deptOrUserKbn){
	var retVal;
	var param1 = document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value;
  	var paramPosition = param1.substring(1);
  	
  	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
  	
	
	prcsKbn = returnAry[0];
	index = returnAry[1];
	deptOrUserKbn = returnAry[2];
	blockKbn = returnAry[3];
  	
  	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
  	
	
	if (typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}

	
	if (retVal[0] == "CLOSE") {
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}

	
	updateCheckInputChange("true");

	
	if (retVal[6] == "") {
		
		
		
		if (deptOrUserKbn == '0') {
			dispInfomation = retVal[4];
		} else {
			dispInfomation = '(' + retVal[4] + ')';
		}
	} else {
		
		dispInfomation = '(' + retVal[4] + ')' + retVal[7];
	}

	if (!blockKbn) {
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonNameDisplay_"+ prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("subPersonNameDisplay_"   + prcsKbn + "_" + index).innerText = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonCode_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonName_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptCode_"         + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptAbbreviation_" + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonNameDisplay_"+ prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("subPersonNameDisplay_"   + prcsKbn + "_" + index).innerText = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonCode_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = retVal[6];
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonName_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = retVal[7];
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptCode_"         + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptAbbreviation_" + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hidOperationStartDate_"  + prcsKbn + "_" + index).value     = "";
		}
	} else {
		if (retVal[0] == "CLEAR") {
			
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonNameDisplay_"+ prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("subPersonNameDisplay_"   + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).innerText = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonCode_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonName_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptCode_"         + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptAbbreviation_" + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;
		} else if (retVal[0] == "SELECT") {
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonNameDisplay_"+ prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("subPersonNameDisplay_"   + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).innerText = dispInfomation;
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonCode_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = retVal[6];
			document.getElementById("hidSubPersonName_"       + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = retVal[7];
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptCode_"         + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = retVal[1];
			document.getElementById("hidSubDeptAbbreviation_" + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = retVal[4];
			document.getElementById("hidOperationStartDate_"  + prcsKbn + "_" + index + "_" + blockKbn).value     = "";
		}
	}

	return true;
}





var isSelectedOkAtCraDeleteDialog = false;


function investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(afterAjPersonCode, isCompleteCancel) {

	
	 
	var causeAnalysisCnt = 1;

	var isDispAlert = false;

	for (var i = 0; i < causeAnalysisCnt; i++) {

		
		var dataKbnElements = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].dataKbn');

		
		if (!dataKbnElements) {
			continue;
		}

		
		if (dataKbnElements[0].value == '3') {
			continue;
		}

		
		var ibcrRaiseDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;

		
		if (ibcrRaiseDeptCode) {
			continue;
		}

		
		var screenElementsDisplayFlag = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].screenElementsDisplayFlag")[0].value;

		
		if (screenElementsDisplayFlag != "0") {
			continue;
		}

		

		
		var beforeInvestigationPersonCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;

		
		var investigationProccessCompleteDate = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

		
		var investigationSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].causeAnalysisResearchSystemSettingCategory")[0].value;

		
		var afterInvestigationPersonCode = beforeInvestigationPersonCode; 
		if (!isCompleteCancel && !investigationProccessCompleteDate && investigationSystemSettingCategory == 'S') {
			

			
			afterInvestigationPersonCode = afterAjPersonCode;
		}


		
		

		
		if (afterAjPersonCode != afterInvestigationPersonCode) {
			
			continue;
		}

		
		if (investigationProccessCompleteDate) {
			

			
			var investigationApprovePersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;

			
			if (!investigationApprovePersonDeptCode) {
				continue;
			}

			
			var investigationApproveCompleteDate = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

			
			if (!investigationApproveCompleteDate) {
				isDispAlert = true;
				break;
			}

		} else {
			
			var isExistsCRA = false;

			for (var k = 0; k < 5; k++) {

				
				var investigationWatchPersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationWatchEntityList[" + k + "].g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim();
				var investigationWatchAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationWatchEntityList[" + k + "].g1AssignProcCategory")[0].value.trim();
				if (investigationWatchPersonDeptCode || investigationWatchAssignProcCategory) {
					isExistsCRA = true;
					break;
				}

				
				var investigationCheckPersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationCheckEntityList[" + k + "].g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim();
				var investigationCheckAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationCheckEntityList[" + k + "].g1AssignProcCategory")[0].value.trim();
				if (investigationCheckPersonDeptCode || investigationCheckAssignProcCategory) {
					isExistsCRA = true;
					break;
				}
			}

			if (!isExistsCRA) {
				
				var investigationApprovePersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim();
				var investigationApproveAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1AssignProcCategory")[0].value.trim();
				if (investigationApprovePersonDeptCode || investigationApproveAssignProcCategory) {
					isExistsCRA = true;
				}
			}

			
			if (isExistsCRA) {
				isDispAlert = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	}



			

		



	return true;
}


function investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4InvestigationAssign(afterInvestigationPersonCode, position, isCompleteCancel) {



	return true;


	
	var ibcrRaiseDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;

	

	

	if (ibcrRaiseDeptCode) {
		return true;
	}

	
	var screenElementsDisplayFlag = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].screenElementsDisplayFlag")[0].value;

	
	if (screenElementsDisplayFlag != "0") {
		return true;
	}

	

	
	var ajPersonCode = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;


	
	

	
	if (ajPersonCode != afterInvestigationPersonCode) {
		
		return true;
	}

	
	var isExistsCRA = false;

	for (var k = 0; k < 5; k++) {

		
		var investigationWatchPersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationWatchEntityList[" + k + "].g1ChargeDeptCode");
		var investigationWatchAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationWatchEntityList[" + k + "].g1AssignProcCategory");
		if ((investigationWatchPersonDeptCode[0] && investigationWatchPersonDeptCode[0].value.trim())
			|| (investigationWatchAssignProcCategory[0] && investigationWatchAssignProcCategory[0].value.trim())) {
			isExistsCRA = true;
			break;
		}

		
		var investigationCheckPersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationCheckEntityList[" + k + "].g1ChargeDeptCode");
		var investigationCheckAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationCheckEntityList[" + k + "].g1AssignProcCategory");
		if ((investigationCheckPersonDeptCode[0] && investigationCheckPersonDeptCode[0].value.trim())
			|| (investigationCheckAssignProcCategory[0] && investigationCheckAssignProcCategory[0].value.trim())) {
			isExistsCRA = true;
			break;
		}
	}

	if (!isExistsCRA) {
		
		var investigationApprovePersonDeptCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode");
		var investigationApproveAssignProcCategory = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1AssignProcCategory");
		if (investigationApprovePersonDeptCode[0].value.trim() || investigationApproveAssignProcCategory[0].value.trim()) {
			isExistsCRA = true;
		}
	}

	
	if (isExistsCRA) {

		var comConfirmResult = false;

		if (isCompleteCancel) {
			

			
			var beforeInvestigationPersonCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;

			if (beforeInvestigationPersonCode != afterInvestigationPersonCode) {
				
				comConfirmResult = comConfirm('YQ10367W:\nAs persons of Investigation and Action Judgement become the same with this operation, \nCheck/ Approval of the Investigation shall be skipped with the checked/ approved data \ndeleted.\nWill you continue this operation accepting the status above?');
			} else {
				
				comConfirmResult = comConfirm('YQ10369W:\nCurrently, persons of Investigation and Action Judgement are the same.\nWith this operation Check/ Approval data shall be deleted \nand so the Check/ Approval shall be skipped then.\nIf you need the Check/ Approval you should change the Investigation person \nor the Action Judgement person beforehand.\nWill you continue this operation without the Check/ Approval?');
			}

		} else {
			
			comConfirmResult = comConfirm('YQ10367W:\nAs persons of Investigation and Action Judgement become the same with this operation, \nCheck/ Approval of the Investigation shall be skipped with the checked/ approved data \ndeleted.\nWill you continue this operation accepting the status above?');
		}

		if (!comConfirmResult) {
			
			return false;
		}

		
		isSelectedOkAtCraDeleteDialog = true;
	}

	return true;
}




function checkCompleteProcessAssginApproval(procCd) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var actionPath = "";
	var completeProcessCode = "";

	var assginCauseAnalysisNo = document.syqaa090Form.assginCauseAnalysisNo.value;


	
	if (procCd == "110") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090E38SUpd.do";
		completeProcessCode = MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE;
	} else if (procCd == "150") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090E92SUpd.do";
		completeProcessCode = MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE;



	} else if (procCd == "135") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090EM4SUpd.do";
		completeProcessCode = MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE;


	} else if (procCd == "140") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090E68SUpd.do";
		completeProcessCode = MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE;

	} else if (procCd == "330") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090EB1SUpd.do";
		completeProcessCode = MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE;


	}


	var ret = checkProcessAssginApproval(completeProcessCode, assginCauseAnalysisNo);

	
	if (ret == false) {
		
		return;
	} else {
		



		if (comSubmit("1")) {
			document.body.style.cursor = 'wait';
			inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";
			document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + actionPath;
			document.forms[0].submit();
		}

	}
}






function checkProcessAssginApproval(completeProcessCode, assginCauseAnalysisNo) {





	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	if (!checkProcessAssginConfirmationAndReference(completeProcessCode, assginCauseAnalysisNo)) {
		return false;
	}


	
	document.getElementsByName("isCheckedApprovalPersonProcessAssign")[0].value = '1';

	
	var targetId;
	var targetName;

	var processCode;
	var assginCauseAnalysisIndex;

	switch (completeProcessCode) {
		
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE:
			
			var element = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ReceivingJudgementCode");
			var value = undefined;
			for (i=0; i<element.length; i++) {
				if (element[i].checked) {
					value = element[i].value; 
					break;
				}
			}
			
			if (!value) {
				return true;
			}
			
			if (value != "2") {
				return true;
			}

			targetId = "apWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = "syqu00ApAPICEntity";

			processCode = MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE;

			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
			targetId = "tjWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = "syqu00TjApprovalPICEntityList";

			processCode = MST_KBN_ACTION_JUDGEMENT;

			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE:
			
			var assginCauseAnalysisIndex = getCauseAnalysisIndex(assginCauseAnalysisNo);
			
			targetId = "";
			targetName = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity";

			processCode = MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE;

			break;


		
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE:
			

			assginCauseAnalysisIndex = getCauseAnalysisIndex(assginCauseAnalysisNo);

			
			targetId = "ivWithoutApprovalFlg_" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex;
			targetName = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].investigationApproveEntity";

			processCode = MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION;

			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
			targetId = "fbWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = "feedbackApproveChargePersonViewEntity";

			processCode = MST_KBN_FEEDBACK;

			break;
		default:
			return true;
			break;
	}

	

	if (document.getElementById(targetId) && document.getElementById(targetId).checked) {

		return true;
	}
	
	
	if ('1' != document.getElementsByName(targetName + ".g1AssignCategory")[0].value) {
		
		return true;
	}
	
	
	var assignedProcess = document.getElementsByName(targetName + ".g1AssignProcCategory")[0].value;
	
	


	var retValue = getProcessChargePersonInfo(processCode, assginCauseAnalysisIndex, assignedProcess, true, false);


	return retValue != undefined;
}



function checkProcessAssginConfirmationAndReference(completeProcessCode, assginCauseAnalysisNo) {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}

	
	document.getElementsByName("isCheckedConfirmationPersonProcessAssign")[0].value = '1';
	
	document.getElementsByName("isCheckedReferencePersonProcessAssign")[0].value = '1';

	for (k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
	
	var targetId;
	var targetName;
	var targetNameC;
	var targetNameR;
	var targetNameColor;
	var targetLabel;
	var targetClick;
	var targetOpen;
	var targetColor;

	var assginCauseAnalysisIndex;

	
	switch (completeProcessCode) {
		
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE:
			
			var element = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ReceivingJudgementCode");
			var value = undefined;
			for (i=0; i<element.length; i++) {
				if (element[i].checked) {
					value = element[i].value; 
					break;
				}
			}
			
			if (!value) {
				continue;
			}
			
			if (value != "2") {
				continue;
			}

			targetId = "apWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = (0==k) ? "syqu00ApCPICEntity" : "syqu00ApRPICEntity";
			targetNameC = "syqu00ApCPICEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameR = "syqu00ApRPICEntity_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameColor = (0==k) ? targetNameC : targetNameR;
			targetColor = "#f7f7f7";
			targetLabel = "#AcceptWf";
			targetClick = "CHECKAPPROVELink";
			targetOpen = "CHECKAPPROVE";

			processCode = MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE;

			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
			targetId = "tjWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = (0==k) ? "syqu00TjConfirmationPICEntityList" : "syqu00TjCheckPICEntityList";
			targetNameC = "syqu00TjConfirmationPICEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameR = "syqu00TjCheckPICEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameColor = (0==k) ? targetNameC : targetNameR;
			targetColor = "#f7f7f7";
			targetLabel = "#TreatmentJudgment";
			targetClick = "CHECKAPPROVE2Link";
			targetOpen = "CHECKAPPROVE2";

			processCode = MST_KBN_ACTION_JUDGEMENT;

			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE:
			
			var assginCauseAnalysisIndex = getCauseAnalysisIndex(assginCauseAnalysisNo);
			
			targetId = "";
			targetName = (0==k) ? "ibcrKianConfirmEntityList" : "ibcrKianReferenceEntityList";
			targetName = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]." + targetName;
			targetNameC = "ibcrKianConfirmEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]";
			targetNameR = "ibcrKianReferenceEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]";
			targetNameColor = (0==k) ? targetNameC : targetNameR;
			targetColor = "#ffffcc";
			var wIdx = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].g1CauseAnalysisNo")[0].value.substr(2,2);
			targetLabel = "#IBCR_RAISE_" + wIdx;
			targetClick = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].CHECKAPPROVE_IBCR_RAISELink";
			targetOpen = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].CHECKAPPROVE_IBCR_RAISE";

			processCode = MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE;

			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE:
			

			assginCauseAnalysisIndex = getCauseAnalysisIndex(assginCauseAnalysisNo);

			
			targetId = "";
			targetName = (0==k) ? "investigationWatchEntityList" : "investigationCheckEntityList";
			targetName = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]." + targetName;
			targetNameC = "investigationWatchEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]";
			targetNameR = "investigationCheckEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "]";
			targetNameColor = (0==k) ? targetNameC : targetNameR;
			targetColor = "#ffffcc";
			var wIdx = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].g1CauseAnalysisNo")[0].value.substr(2,2);
			targetLabel = "#InvestigationWf_" + wIdx;
			targetClick = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].CHECKAPPROVE_INVESTIGATIONLink";
			targetOpen = "causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + assginCauseAnalysisIndex + "].CHECKAPPROVE_INVESTIGATION";

			processCode = MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION;

			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
			targetId = "fbWithoutApprovalFlg";
			targetName = (0==k) ? "feedbackCheckChargePersonViewEntityList" : "feedbackReferenceChargePersonViewEntityList";
			targetNameC = "feedbackCheckChargePersonViewEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameR = "feedbackReferenceChargePersonViewEntityList_g1ChargePersonNameDisplay";
			targetNameColor = (0==k) ? targetNameC : targetNameR;
			targetColor = "#f7f7f7";
			targetLabel = "#FeedbackWf";
			targetClick = "FEEDBACKCHECKAPPROVELink";
			targetOpen = "FEEDBACKCHECKAPPROVE";

			processCode = MST_KBN_FEEDBACK;

			break;
		default:
			return true;
			break;
	}

	
	
	
	if (1 == k && document.getElementById(targetId) && document.getElementById(targetId).checked) {
		continue;
	}
	
	
	document.getElementById(targetNameC + "[0]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameC + "[1]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameC + "[2]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameC + "[3]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameC + "[4]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameR + "[0]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameR + "[1]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameR + "[2]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameR + "[3]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
	document.getElementById(targetNameR + "[4]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;

	for (j = 0; j < 5; j++) {
		
		if ('1' == document.getElementsByName(targetName + "["+ j +"].g1AssignCategory")[0].value) {
			
			var assignedProcess = document.getElementsByName(targetName + "["+ j +"].g1AssignProcCategory")[0].value;
			

			var retValue = getProcessChargePersonInfo(processCode, assginCauseAnalysisIndex, assignedProcess, false, true);

			if (retValue == undefined) {
				document.getElementById(targetNameColor + "["+ j +"]").style.backgroundColor = "red";
				
				if (document.getElementById(targetOpen).style.display == "none") {
					document.getElementById(targetClick).click();
				}
				window.location.href = targetLabel;
				var ret = comConfirmYesNo('YQ10351W: The person assigned to the check/ watch as the eventual person of a certain job process name has moved or been deleted.\n\nDo you want to select another person?', 'To Make Any Assignment', VB_DEFAULT_BUTTON1);
				if (ret == CONFIRM_YES) {
					return false;
				} else {
					document.getElementById(targetNameColor + "["+ j +"]").style.backgroundColor = targetColor;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	}
	
	return true;
}





function getCauseAnalysisIndex(causeAnalysisNo) {
	return document.getElementsByName("causeIdx_" + causeAnalysisNo)[0].value;
}





function getProcessChargePersonInfo(processCode, position, selectProcessCode, isApprovePerson, isCOrR) {



	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return undefined;
	}


	
	var candidateList = getCandidateForProcessAssign(processCode, position, selectProcessCode, isApprovePerson);

	var conductionBaseCode = '14';

	
	if (candidateList.length < 1) {

		var msg = getnotGetCandidateMsg(notGetCandidateCode, isApprovePerson);
		alert(msg);

		return undefined;
	}
	
	
	var newPersonList = new Array(0);
	for (var i = 0; i < candidateList.length; i++) {
		var person = candidateList[i];



		var isEffective = '0';
		

		
		if (processCode == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION
			  || processCode == MST_KBN_CHARGE_CAUSE_ANALYSIS_INVESTIGATION
			  || processCode == MST_KBN_COUNTERMEASURE_DECISION
			  || processCode == MST_KBN_ECN
			  || processCode == MST_KBN_ACTION) {
			
			var causeAnalysisBaseCode = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].g1CauseAnalysisBaseCode")[0].value;
			
			if ((causeAnalysisBaseCode == person.baseCode)
			|| (processCode == MST_KBN_ACTION && conductionBaseCode == person.baseCode)) { 
				isEffective = isEffectiveDeptOrUser(person.chargePersonCode, person.chargeDeptCode);
			} else {
				isEffective = isEffectiveDeptOrUserWithOtherProductionBases(person.chargePersonCode, person.chargeDeptCode);
			}
		} else {
			
			if (conductionBaseCode == person.baseCode) {
				isEffective = isEffectiveDeptOrUser(person.chargePersonCode, person.chargeDeptCode);
			} else {
				isEffective = isEffectiveDeptOrUserWithOtherProductionBases(person.chargePersonCode, person.chargeDeptCode);
			}
		}

		if ('1' == isEffective) {
			newPersonList.push(person);
		}
	}


	if (isCOrR) {
		if (newPersonList.length < 1) {
			return undefined;
		} else {
			return true;
		}
	} else {

		
		if (newPersonList.length < 1) {

			
			
			if (isApprovePerson == true) {
				alert('YQ10129E: The Job-process Person assigned the approval to, currently, is not a registered SIFT user, has no authority: Editing Documents or has moved to another base or another Dgt. \r\n Assign the approval to another Job-process Person or by a certain personal name.');
			} else {
				alert('The selected Job-process Person currently, has been deleted, has no authority: Editing Documents or has moved to another base or another Dgt. \r\n Assign it to another Job-process Person or by a certain personal name.');
			}

			return undefined;
		} else if (1 < newPersonList.length) {
			

			
			if (isApprovePerson == true) {
				alert('YQ10295E: Cannot identify one and assign the approval to the job-process person because the job process has plural persons in charge. \r\n Appoint another job-process person or appoint a certain personal name.');
			} else {

				alert("YQ10298E: Cannot assign it to the job-process person because plural persons in charge exist of the selected job process.\r\n Select another job process or appoint a certain personal name.");

			}

			return undefined;
		}

	}

		
	
	var returnValue = new Object();
	returnValue.chargePersonCode = newPersonList[0].chargePersonCode; 
	returnValue.chargePersonName = newPersonList[0].chargePersonName; 
	returnValue.chargeDeptCode = newPersonList[0].chargeDeptCode; 
	returnValue.chargeDeptAbbreviation = newPersonList[0].chargeDeptAbbreviation; 
	returnValue.chargePersonNameDisplay = newPersonList[0].chargePersonNameDisplay; 
	
	return returnValue;
}

function checkUpdate() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return;
	}



	
	if (document.body.style.checkInputChange == "true") {
		document.syqaa090Form.windowUpdatedFlg.value = '1';
	}


};



function getnotGetCandidateMsg(notGetCandidateCode, isApprovePerson) {
	if (notGetCandidateCode == notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver) {
		
		if (isApprovePerson) {
			return "YQ10305E: You can not appoint this job-process person as the approver, because he/she does not belong to the same base as the person who is currently in charge of this job process belongs. \r\nAppoint another job-process person or a certain personal name.";
		} else {
			return "YQ10306E: You can not appoint this job-process person, because he/she does not belong to the same base as the person who is currently in charge of this job process belongs. \r\nAppoint another job-process person or a certain personal name.";
		}
	}
	
	
	if (isApprovePerson) {
		return "YQ10292E: The appointed job-process person doesn't exist currently or approver assignment by the job-process person is refused. \r\n Appoint another job-process person or a certain personal name.";
	} else {
		return "YQ10297E: The selected Job-process Person currently, has been deleted, has no authority: Editing Documents or has moved to another base or another Dgt. \r\n Assign it to another Job-process Person or by a certain personal name.";
	}
	
}
	


var notGetCandidateCode = "";

var notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver = "a";	



function getCandidateForProcessAssign(processCode, position, selectProcessCode, isApprovePerson) {


	
	notGetCandidateCode = "";


	var personList = new Array();
	
	var getTargetBase; 
	var isAssignInYellowFace; 
	var isBaseCanStepOver; 

	if (processCode == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION
		  || processCode == MST_KBN_CHARGE_CAUSE_ANALYSIS_INVESTIGATION
		  || processCode == MST_KBN_COUNTERMEASURE_DECISION
		  || processCode == MST_KBN_ECN
		  || processCode == MST_KBN_ACTION) {
		
		getTargetBase = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + position + "].g1CauseAnalysisBaseCode")[0].value;
		
		isAssignInYellowFace = true; 

		if (processCode == MST_KBN_ACTION) {
			isBaseCanStepOver = true; 
		} else {
			isBaseCanStepOver = false; 
		}

		


	} else {
		
		getTargetBase = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
		
		isAssignInYellowFace = false; 


		
		isBaseCanStepOver = false; 


	}

	
	if (selectProcessCode == '110') {
		
		var person = getChargePerson("syqu00ApPICEntity", isApprovePerson);

		if (!person) {
			return personList;
		}

		
		if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
			personList.push(person);

		} else {
			
			notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

		}

	} else if (selectProcessCode == '130') {
		
		var person = getChargePerson("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity", isApprovePerson);

		if (!person) {
			return personList;

		}

		
		if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
			personList.push(person);

		} else {
			
			notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

		}


	} else if (selectProcessCode == '135') {
		

		
		 
		var causeAnalysisCnt = 1;
		
		for (var i = 0; i < causeAnalysisCnt; i++) {

			var dataKbnElements = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].dataKbn');
			if (dataKbnElements) {
				
				if (dataKbnElements[0].value == '3') {
					continue;
				}

				var person = getChargePerson("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity", isApprovePerson);



				if (!person) {
					continue;
				}
		
				
				if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
					personList.push(person);

				} else {
					
					notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

				}
			}

		}

	} else if (selectProcessCode == '140') {
		
		
		
		
		 
		var causeAnalysisCnt = 1;
		
		for (var i = 0; i < causeAnalysisCnt; i++) {
			
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].g1CauseAnalysisBaseCode")[0].value != getTargetBase) {
				continue;
			}

			
			if (isAssignInYellowFace && position != i) {


				continue;
			}

			var dataKbnElements = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].dataKbn');
			if (dataKbnElements) {
				
				if (dataKbnElements[0].value == '3') {


					continue;
				}

				var person = getChargePerson("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity", isApprovePerson);

				if (!person) {
					continue;
				}
		
				
				if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
					personList.push(person);

				} else {
					
					notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

				}
			}
		}

	} else if (selectProcessCode == '150') {
		
		var person = getChargePerson("syqu00TjPICEntityList", isApprovePerson);

		if (!person) {
			return personList;
		}

		
		if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
			personList.push(person);

		} else {
			
			notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

		}

	} else if (selectProcessCode == '330') {
		
		var person = getChargePerson("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity", isApprovePerson);

		if (!person) {
			return personList;
		}

		
		if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
			personList.push(person);

		} else {
			
			notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

		}

	} else if (selectProcessCode == '500') {
		
		
		
		 
		var meetingBaseCnt = 1;
	
		
		for (i = 0; i < meetingBaseCnt; i++) {
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value != getTargetBase) {
				continue;
			}

			
			if (!document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].g1DeliberationFlg")
				|| !document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].g1DeliberationFlg")[0].checked) {
				continue;
			}

			
			var reportChargeDeptCntOfEachBase = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ReportChargeDeptViewEntityListSize_" + i)[0].value;
			
			for (j = 0; j < reportChargeDeptCntOfEachBase; j++) {

				
				if (!document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].syqaa090ReportChargeDeptViewEntity["+j+"].dataKbn")
					|| document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].syqaa090ReportChargeDeptViewEntity["+j+"].dataKbn")[0].value == 3) {
					continue;
				}

				var person = getChargePerson("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList["+i+"].syqaa090ReportChargeDeptViewEntity["+j+"].reportChargePerson", isApprovePerson);

				if (!person) {
					continue;
				}
		
				
				if (isBaseCanStepOver || getTargetBase == person.baseCode) {
					personList.push(person);

				} else {
					
					notGetCandidateCode = notGetCandidateCode_baseStepOver;

				}

			}
			
			break;
		}
	}

	
	var filterPersonList = new Array();
	for (var i = 0; i < personList.length; i++) {

		var person = personList[i];
		var j = 0;
		for (; j < filterPersonList.length; j++) {
			if (person.chargePersonCode == filterPersonList[j].chargePersonCode
				&& person.chargeDeptCode == filterPersonList[j].chargeDeptCode) {
				break;
			}
		}

		
		if (filterPersonList.length <= j) {
			
			if ('' == person.chargePersonCode || '1' == isProcessAssignableUser(person.chargePersonCode, selectProcessCode, person.proccessCompleteDate)) {
				filterPersonList.push(person);


			}
		}
	}

	return filterPersonList;
}


function getChargePerson(name, isApprovePerson) {
	
	var person = {
		  chargePersonCode			: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value 
		, chargePersonName			: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ChargePersonName")[0].value 
		, chargeDeptCode			: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value 
		, chargeDeptAbbreviation 	: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ChargeDeptAbbreviation")[0].value 
		, chargePersonNameDisplay	: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ChargePersonNameDisplay")[0].value 
		, baseCode					: '' 												
		, ignoreFlg					: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1IgnoreFlg")[0].value 
		, proccessCompleteDate		: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value 

		, systemSettingCategory		: document.getElementsByName(name + ".g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value 

	}

	
	if ('' == person.chargePersonCode && '' == person.chargeDeptCode) {
		return undefined;
	}

	
	if (isApprovePerson) {
		if ('' == person.chargePersonCode) {


			return undefined;
		}
	}

	
	if ('1' == person.ignoreFlg) {
		return undefined;
	}
	
	
	person.baseCode = getDeptUserBaseCode('' != person.chargePersonCode ? person.chargePersonCode : person.chargeDeptCode);
	if ('' == person.baseCode && '' == person.chargePersonCode) {
		person.baseCode = person.chargeDeptCode.substring(0,2);
	}
	
	return person;
}

function searchBundingPutting() {


	if (!submitFlg) {
		return;
	}



	/** 既に直接入力ウィンドウが存在する場合、ウィンドウを閉じる */
	closeAnotherWindow("SYQAA890" + "IN202310B06811");



	
	var g1ConductionBaseCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseCode").value;

	
	var g1ManufactureFactoryCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ManufactureFactoryCode").value;

	if (g1ManufactureFactoryCode.trim() == "") {
		alert('YQ10112E: You need to set the production base on SEARCH FOR THE SUPERSESSION. Input it.');
		return;
	}

	
	var g1ProductCategory = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ProductCategory").value;
	

    
	var g1ProductModelCodeSumOfFtir = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ProductModelCodeSum").value;
	
    var g1CausalPartsNoOfFtir = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1CausalPartsNoToBundleSearch").value;



	
    var g1JudgementTempCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value;


	
	var qs = {

	 g1ConductionBaseCode: g1ConductionBaseCode,

	 g1ManufactureFactoryCode: g1ManufactureFactoryCode,

	 g1ProductCategory: g1ProductCategory,
	 g1ProductModelCodeSumOfFtir: g1ProductModelCodeSumOfFtir,

	 g1CausalPartsNoOfFtir: g1CausalPartsNoOfFtir,

	 g1JudgementCode: g1JudgementTempCode

	};
	
	var windowName = 'SYQAA120' + 'IN202310B06811';

	
	var width = '1008';
	var heigth = '-1';

	comOpenWindow('/SYQAA120E00SFnd', windowName, qs, width, heigth);

}


function g1PendingFlgClick(index) {
	
	var baseCode = document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList[" + index + "].g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
	var pendingFlg = $("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode);
	
	
 
	if (baseCode == $("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseCode").value
		&& $('fpcrDeliberationFlg').checked) {
 
		$('fpcrPendingFlg').checked = pendingFlg.checked
		siftCtrl.changeCheck($('fpcrPendingFlg'));
		
		
		if (!$('fpcrPendingFlgHidden')) {
			
		} else if ($('fpcrPendingFlg').checked) {
			$('fpcrPendingFlgHidden').value = "1";
		} else {
			$('fpcrPendingFlgHidden').value = "0";
		}
	}
}



function defaultPendingDate(index) {
	
	var baseCode = document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList[" + index + "].g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
	var pendingFlg = $("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode);
	var judgCode1 = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1RankPrimaryJudgement")[0].value;
	var judgCode2 = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1RankSecondaryJudgement")[0].value;

	if (14 != baseCode) {
		return;
	}
	if (pendingFlg.checked) {
		if (judgCode1 != "" || judgCode2 != "") {
			
			pendingDateCalculate(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1ReportDate")[0].value, baseCode);
		}
	} else {
		$("pendDate_"  + baseCode).innerText = ""; 
		$("hdnPendDate_" + baseCode).value = "";
	}
}

		} else {

		}
	}
}

	if (document.getElementById(id).checked) {
		
		alert('YQ10343W: You put check on S Matter checkbox.');
	} else {
