	}
}
 


function g1DeliberationFlgClick(index) {
	
	var baseCode = document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList[" + index + "].g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
	var deliberationFlg = document.getElementById("g1DeliberationFlg_span_" + baseCode);

	
	strategyMeetingResultControl(index);
	
	
	if (baseCode == $("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseCode").value) {
		
		if ($('deliberationRecNo_hidden_' + baseCode).value.trim() != "") {
			$('fpcrDeliberationFlg').disabled = true;
			siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrDeliberationFlg'));		
		} else {


				
				$('fpcrDeliberationFlg').disabled = ($('fpcrDeliberationFlg').style.defaultDisabled == "true");
				siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrDeliberationFlg'));


		}
		
		
		$('fpcrDeliberationFlg').checked = deliberationFlg.checked;
		siftCtrl.changeCheck($('fpcrDeliberationFlg'));

 
		fpcrDeliberationFlgControl();
 
	}
}

function strategyMeetingResultControl(index) {

	var baseCode = document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList[" + index + "].g1ConductionBaseCode")[0].value;
	var deliberationFlg = document.getElementById("g1DeliberationFlg_span_" + baseCode);
	var deliberationConcludeFlg = document.getElementById('g1DeliberationConcludedFlg_' + baseCode).value;

	var meetingStatus = document.getElementsByName("strategyMeetingResultViewEntityList[" + index + "].deliberationStatusNameKey")[0].value;
	var pendingFlg = document.getElementById("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode); 


	var strategyMeetingResult1 = document.getElementById("strategyMeetingResult1_" + index);
	var strategyMeetingResult2 = document.getElementById("strategyMeetingResult2_" + index);
	var strategyMeetingResult3 = document.getElementById("strategyMeetingResult3_" + index);
	var strategyMeetingResultInputButton = document.getElementById("strategyMeetingResultInputButton");

	if (deliberationFlg && (deliberationFlg.checked || (deliberationFlg.type == 'hidden' && deliberationFlg.value == '1'))) {
		strategyMeetingResult1.style.display = "";
		strategyMeetingResult2.style.display = "";
 
		strategyMeetingResult3.style.display = "";
 

 
		
 

	} else {
		strategyMeetingResult1.style.display = "none";
		strategyMeetingResult2.style.display = "none";
		strategyMeetingResult3.style.display = "none";
	}

	
	if ((baseCode == '14')
	  && (deliberationFlg && !deliberationFlg.disabled)) {

		if (deliberationFlg && (deliberationFlg.checked || (deliberationFlg.type == 'hidden' && deliberationFlg.value == '1'))) {
			strategyMeetingResultInputButton.disabled = (strategyMeetingResultInputButton.style.defaultDisabled == "true");
		} else {
			strategyMeetingResultInputButton.disabled = true;
		}
 


		if (deliberationFlg && (deliberationFlg.checked || (deliberationFlg.type == 'hidden' && deliberationFlg.value == '1'))) {
			
			pendingFlg.disabled = false;
			siftCtrl.changeDisable(document.getElementById("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode));

			document.getElementById("pendCalButton_" + baseCode).disabled = false;

		} else {
			
			pendingFlg.checked = false;
			siftCtrl.changeCheck(document.getElementById("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode));
			pendingFlg.disabled = true;
			siftCtrl.changeDisable(document.getElementById("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode));

			document.getElementById("pendDate_" + baseCode).innerText = "";
			document.getElementById("hdnPendDate_" + baseCode).value = "";
			document.getElementById("pendCalButton_" + baseCode).disabled = true;

		}
 
	}

	if (baseCode == '14') {
		if (meetingStatus == "L00216") {
			
			pendingFlg.disabled = true;
			document.getElementById("pendCalButton_" + baseCode).disabled = true;
			siftCtrl.changeDisable(document.getElementById("deliberationPendingFlg_" + baseCode));
		}
	}

}



var MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE = "1";

var MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE = "2";

var MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE = "3";

var MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE = "4";

var MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE = "5";


var MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE = "6";



var MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE = "7";



function checkComplete(mst_kbn, filterBaseCode, action) {



	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	var isCheckAlert = '0';
	if (isCheckAlert == '1') {


		return true;
	}


	
	if (mst_kbn.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE) {
		
		if ('2' == document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value) {
			
			if (!investigationAllCompleteCheck()) {
				var ret = confirm('YQ10377E: You are making Assumed Completion on Action Judgement. Collecting Information and Investigation shall not be automatically completed, with continuing. You should complete them manually.');
				if (!ret) {
					return false;
				}
			}
		}
	}


	var person = "";
	document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '0';


	
	document.getElementsByName("isCheckedApprovalPersonProcessAssign")[0].value = '0';


	switch (mst_kbn.charAt(0)) {
		
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE:

			
			if (!confirmIbcrRaise(mst_kbn.substring(1))) {
				
				document.body.style.cursor = "default";
				return false;
			}

			
			if (document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
				&& document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			} else {
				
				person = document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
			}
			break;

		default:
			break;
	}




	var assginCauseAnalysisNo = document.syqaa090Form.assginCauseAnalysisNo.value;


	
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	
	var returnAry = [];
	returnAry.push(mst_kbn, filterBaseCode, action);
	
	document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();
	
	if (document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value != person.trim()) {

		
		var paramAry = {};
		paramAry["messageId"] = "YQ10028W";
		paramAry["buttonId"] = btnYes + "," + btnNo + "," + btnCancel;
		
		comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, 'checkComplete', 460, 150);
		return false;
		

	} else {
		isPersonChanged2 = false;		

		
		if (document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value == '0') {
			

			if (!checkProcessAssginApproval(mst_kbn.charAt(0), assginCauseAnalysisNo)) {

				document.body.style.cursor = "default";
				return false;
			}

		} else {
			
			if (filterBaseCode != undefined && filterBaseCode != '14') {
				alert('YQ10282E: You cannot replace the person in charge and complete this job process because you belong to a different base from him/ her.');
				document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
				document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
				return false;
			}



				






			if (mst_kbn.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE) {
				
				
				var ajSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				var infoSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				if (infoSystemSettingCategory == 'S'
					&& ajSystemSettingCategory == 'S') {
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
						document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}
			}

			if (mst_kbn.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE) {
				
				
				var ajSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				if (ajSystemSettingCategory == 'S') {
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
						document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}
			}

			if (mst_kbn.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE) {
				
				if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
					document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = "";
					document.body.style.cursor = "default";
					return false;
				}
			}


		}

	}
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
	
	checkCompleteReturn2(false);
}




function checkCompleteReturn(){
	var assginCauseAnalysisNo = document.syqaa090Form.assginCauseAnalysisNo.value;

	
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
	
	var mst_kbn = returnAry[0];
	var filterBaseCode = returnAry[1];
	var action = returnAry[2];
	
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	

//switch (comConfirmYesNoCancel('YQ10028W: The person in charge of this job process is another than the one (yourself) who is now completing this. Will you change the charged person of this to the one (yourself)?')) {
	switch (retVal[0]) {


	case btnYes:


		
		if (filterBaseCode != "" && filterBaseCode != '14') {
			alert('YQ10282E: You cannot replace the person in charge and complete this job process because you belong to a different base from him/ her.');
			document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
			return false;
		}


		switch (mst_kbn.charAt(0)) {
			
			case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE:

				
				
				var ajSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				var infoSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				if (infoSystemSettingCategory == 'S'
					&& ajSystemSettingCategory == 'S') {
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}


				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApPICEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;
			
			case MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE:

				
				
				var ajSystemSettingCategory = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1SystemSettingCategory")[0].value;
				if (ajSystemSettingCategory == 'S') {
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}


				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;
			
			case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE:
				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;
			
			case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;
			
			case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:

				
				if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value, false)) {
					document.body.style.cursor = "default";
					return false;
				}


				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;

			
			case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE:
				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + mst_kbn.substring(1) + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;


			
			case MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE:
				returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
				chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("tsrChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
				orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
				break;

			default:
				break;
		}
		document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
		break;
	case btnNo:
		
		isPersonChanged = false;

		

		if (!checkProcessAssginApproval(mst_kbn.charAt(0), assginCauseAnalysisNo)) {

			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;
		}

		break;
	case btnCancel:
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	default:
		break;
	}
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
	
	checkCompleteReturn2(false);	
}


function checkCompleteReturn2(previousDepartmentFlg){
	
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
	
	var mst_kbn = returnAry[0];
	var filterBaseCode = returnAry[1];
	var action = returnAry[2];
	
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	
	if(previousDepartmentFlg){
		//switch (comConfirmYesNoCancel('YQ10040W: You can transact it as a member of your ex-Dgt. Select "Yes" when as the ex-Dgt, select "No" when as the current Dgt or select "Cancel" when you cancel it.', 'Assign')) {
		switch (retVal[0]) {
		case btnYes:
			
			document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '1';
			break;
		case btnNo:
			
			document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
			break;
		case btnCancel:
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			if (isPersonChanged && isPersonChanged2) {		
				
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = orgAssignUserIdElement.value;
			}
			return false;
		default:
			break;
		}
	}

	
	if (mst_kbn.charAt(0) == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE) {
		if (document.getElementById("impossibleToAscertainFlg_" + mst_kbn.substring(1)).checked == false
				&& document.getElementById("noResponsibilityFlg_" + mst_kbn.substring(1)).checked == false
				&& document.getElementById("substantialFlg_" + mst_kbn.substring(1)).checked == false
				&& document.getElementById("noResponsibilityBaseFlg_" + mst_kbn.substring(1)).checked == false) {
			alert('YQ10052I:\nAs you complete INVESTIGATION, you continue following job processes in FPCR after its issue.\nThe FPCR will be issued by the person of ACTION JUDGEMENT based on your Investigation Result.\n\nUtilize fields of ROOT CAUSE ANALYSIS, SETTLEMENT OF C\'MEASURE COURSE etc.\nshowing up after this, when you have input items preliminarily before the FPCR issue.\nThe FPCR takes over these inputted items in the FTIR.');
		}
	}



	
	document.getElementsByName("wishEntryProcessCd")[0].value = '';

	
	if (document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ReceivingJudgementCode")[0].value == '1'
		&& document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionReceivedStatusView")[0].value.trim() == "") {
		var chkProcUserId = "";
		var dialogMessage = "";
		var wishProcCd = "";
		var isWishConfirm = true;

		
		switch (mst_kbn.charAt(0)) {
			
			case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
				chkProcUserId = document.getElementsByName("feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
				dialogMessage = 'You are completing Individual Reply, without the parts retrieved. The person of this Individual Reply shall have this FTIR in his/ her Wish List of Incident Parts. Are you sure about that?\nWithout having this FTIR in the Wish List, the parts coming in the future will never be announced for him/ her.\nWith possible change of Individual Reply after the parts coming, you are strongly recommended to have this FTIR in the Wish List.';
				wishProcCd = '330';
				break;
			
			case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
				chkProcUserId = document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
				dialogMessage = 'You are completing Action Judgement, without the parts retrieved. The person of this Action Judgement shall have this FTIR in his/ her Wish List of Incident Parts. Are you sure about that?\nWithout having this FTIR in the Wish List, the parts coming in the future will never be announced for him/ her.\nWith possible change of Investigation/ Action Judgement after the parts coming, you are strongly recommended to have this FTIR in the Wish List.';
				wishProcCd = '150';
				break;
			default:
				break;
		}
	
		
		if (chkProcUserId == "") {
			isWishConfirm = false;
		} else {
			
			 
			var wishCnt = 0;
			
			for (var i = 0; i < wishCnt; i++) {
				if (document.getElementsByName("syqaa090WishUserViewEntityList[" + i + "].g1WishUserId")[0].value.trim() == chkProcUserId) {
				
					isWishConfirm = false;
				}
			}
		}
	

		isWishConfirm = false;
		
		
		if (isWishConfirm) {
			switch (comConfirmYesNo(dialogMessage, 'WISH LIST OF INCIDENT PARTS', VB_DEFAULT_BUTTON2)) {
				case CONFIRM_YES:
					
					document.getElementsByName("wishEntryProcessCd")[0].value = wishProcCd;
					break;
				case CONFIRM_NO:
					
					break;
				default:
					break;
			}
		}
	}



	var g1JudgementTempCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value;
	switch (mst_kbn.charAt(0)) {
		
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
			
			var conductionInfoJson = getSbFpcrConductionCode(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession")[0].value);
			
			if (conductionInfoJson != "") {
				var conductionInfo = eval("(" + conductionInfoJson + ")");
				if (('14' != conductionInfo.conductCode) && ("" != conductionInfo.conductCode)) {
					
					if (g1JudgementTempCode == 2) {
						
						var conductionMessage = 'The supersession destination FPCR (SBPR) is of the transaction base, '+ conductionInfo.conductName +'.';

						
						conductionMessage = conductionMessage.replace(/\.\./g, '\.');
						

/* 						switch (comConfirmOkCancel(conductionMessage, 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {
							case CONFIRM_OK:
								break;
							case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
								document.body.style.cursor = "default";
								return false;
							default:
								break;
						} 
*/
						if(confirm(conductionMessage)){
							break;
						} else {
							document.body.style.cursor = "default";
							return false;
						}

					}
					if (g1JudgementTempCode == 3) {
						
						var conductionMessage = 'The supersession destination FPCR (SBPR) is of the transaction base, '+ conductionInfo.conductName +'.\n';
						var conductionMessage1 = 'With this transaction, transaction base of the supersession destination SBPR shall transfer from '+ conductionInfo.conductName +' to MARUTI SUZUKI INDIA LIMITED.';

						conductionMessage += conductionMessage1;

						
						conductionMessage = conductionMessage.replace(/\.\./g, '\.');


/* 						switch (comConfirmOkCancel(conductionMessage, 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {
							case CONFIRM_OK:
								break;
							case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
								document.body.style.cursor = "default";
								return false;
							default:
								break;
						} 
*/
						if(confirm(conductionMessage)){
							break;
						} else {
							document.body.style.cursor = "default";
							return false;
						}
						
					}
										
				} else {
					
					if (('123500' != conductionInfo.manufactureFactoryCode) && ("" != conductionInfo.manufactureFactoryCode)) {
						

						if (g1JudgementTempCode == 2) {

							var manufactureFactoryMessage = 'This FTIR differs from the supersession destination FPCR (SBPR) regarding their production base.Production base of supersession destination is '+ conductionInfo.manufactureFactoryName +'.';

							
							manufactureFactoryMessage = manufactureFactoryMessage.replace(/\.\./g, '\.');


/*							 switch (comConfirmOkCancel(manufactureFactoryMessage, 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {
								case CONFIRM_OK:
									break;
								case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
									document.body.style.cursor = "default";
									return false;
								default:
									break;
							} 
*/
							if(confirm(manufactureFactoryMessage)){
								break;
							} else {
								document.body.style.cursor = "default";
								return false;
							}
							
						}
						
						if (g1JudgementTempCode == 3) {

							var manufactureFactoryMessage = 'This FTIR differs from the supersession destination FPCR (SBPR) regarding their production base.Production base of supersession destination is '+ conductionInfo.manufactureFactoryName +'.\n';
							var manufactureFactoryMessage1 = 'With this transaction, production base of the supersession destination SBPR shall transfer from '+ conductionInfo.manufactureFactoryName +' to MARUTI SUZUKI INDIA LIMITED.';
							manufactureFactoryMessage += manufactureFactoryMessage1;

							
							manufactureFactoryMessage = manufactureFactoryMessage.replace(/\.\./g, '\.');


/* 							switch (comConfirmOkCancel(manufactureFactoryMessage, 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {
								case CONFIRM_OK:
									break;
								case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
									document.body.style.cursor = "default";
									return false;
								default:
									break;
							} 
*/
							if(confirm(manufactureFactoryMessage)){
								break;
							} else {
								document.body.style.cursor = "default";
								return false;
							}

						}
					}
				}	

			}
			break;
		default:
			break;
	}



	//action先を呼び出し元から渡せないので、再設定
	switch (mst_kbn.charAt(0)) {
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090E38SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "//SYQAA090E50SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090E68SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EB1SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090E92SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EM4SUpd.do";
			break;
		case MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE:
			document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EQ2SUpd.do";
			break;
	}
	if (action == "/SYQAA090EQ4SUpd") {
		document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EQ4SUpd.do";
	}
	if(comSubmit("1")){
		document.syqaa090Form.submit();
	}
	return true;

}
	


function confirmIbcrRaise(causeAnalysisIndex) {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	
	var ibcrKianApprovedOnce = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + causeAnalysisIndex + '].ibcrKianApprovedOnce')[0].value;
	return (ibcrKianApprovedOnce == "true" || comConfirm('YQ10280W: You cannot change the IBCR destination base even with canceling the execution. Are you sure to execute the IBCR?'));
}


function showPartsSendingForm(ftirNo) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var qs = {"g1Id" : ftirNo};
	comDownloadSubmit('/PYQAA110E00SDld', qs);
}


function confirmIbcrApprove(causeAnalysisIndex) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	if (!confirmIbcrRaise(causeAnalysisIndex)) {
		
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}

	var userId = $val("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + causeAnalysisIndex + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity.g1ChargePersonCode");
	var deptCd = $val("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + causeAnalysisIndex + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode");

	
	if (!checkCompleteCancelMain(userId, MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_A, deptCd)) {
		return false;
	}

	return checkOldDeptUseAndCancel();
}


function checkCancel() {


	return checkOldDeptUseAndCancel();

}

function checkOldDeptUseAndCancel() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	if (comCheckInputChange()) {
		
		if (comConfirm('YQ10035W: Some data in the view has been revised. Are you sure to save the revision?')) {
			if (!checkOldDeptUse()) {
				return false;
			}
			

			inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";

			return true;
		}
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}
	if (!checkOldDeptUse()) {
		return false;
	}
	

	inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";

	return true;
}

function checkOldDeptUse() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
	
	return true;
}



var MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "1";

var MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "2";

var MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "3";

var MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "4";

var MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "5";


var MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "6";



var MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE_CANCEL = "7";




function replaceInfo() {
	this.replaceFlg = "0";		
	this.targetProcess = "";					
	this.targetProcessExt = "";					
	
	this.currentUserId = "";					
	this.currentDeptCode = "";					
	
	this.newUserId = "";						
	this.newDeptCode = "";						
	
	this.newProcess = "";						
	

	this.addMemoCommentFlg = "0"; 

}


function getReplaceInfo() {
	var obj = new replaceInfo();
	
	
	obj.targetProcess = arguments[0];
	obj.targetProcessExt = arguments[1];
	obj.currentUserId = arguments[2];
	obj.currentDeptCode = arguments[3];
	
	if (arguments.length <= 4) {
		
		obj.newUserId = "INA28423";
		obj.newDeptCode = "1400553";
	} else {
		obj.newUserId = arguments[4].trim();
		obj.newDeptCode = arguments[5];
		obj.newProcess = arguments[6];

		obj.mstKbn = arguments[7];

	}
	
	return obj;
}


function claerSYQAA090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityHidden() {
	var hiddenSpan = document.getElementById("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityListSpan");
	while(hiddenSpan.firstChild) {
		hiddenSpan.removeChild(hiddenSpan.firstChild);
	}
}


function addSYQAA090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityHidden(obj, idx) {
	
	var namePrefix = "syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[" + idx + "].";	
	var hiddenSpan = document.getElementById("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityListSpan");
	var hdn;
	
	


	
	
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'targetProcessCode" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.targetProcess + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'targetProcessCodeExtension" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.targetProcessExt + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'changeUserId" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.newUserId + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'changeDeptCd" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.newDeptCode + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'changeProcessCode" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.newProcess + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'replaceFlg" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.replaceFlg + '">';
		hiddenSpan.innerHTML += '<input type="hidden" name="' + namePrefix + 'addMemoCommentFlg" style="ignoreCheck: true;" value="' + obj.addMemoCommentFlg + '">';
	

}

	return document.getElementsByName(arguments[0])[0].value
}


function rcNoTolistIndex(rcNo) {

}

function changeUnEffectiveChargeDeptOrPerson() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var targetList = new Array();

	var extensionWork = ''

		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
		
			if (receivingJudgementCode == '1') {
				
				targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('110', extensionDef, arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			} else {

					$val('syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'), $val('syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode')));
				
				
					
					
					
					
					extensionWork = '01';
						
						
					
					targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('140', extensionWork,
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode')));
						
					
					targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('141', extensionWork,
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqu00ChargePersonEndViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqu00ChargePersonEndViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode')));
					
					targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('142', extensionWork,
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqu00DecisionChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqu00DecisionChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode')));

						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqaa170ApprOneECNViewEntity.userCode'),
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqaa170ApprOneECNViewEntity.deptCode')));
					
					targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('144', extensionWork,
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqaa170ApprOneActionViewEntity.userCode'),
						$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[0].syqaa170ApprOneActionViewEntity.deptCode')));
					
				
				
				
				targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('150', extensionDef,
					$val('syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode'), $val('syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargeDeptCode')));
				
				targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('330', extensionDef,
					$val('feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'), $val('feedbackChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode')));
			}
			
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_INFORMATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('130', extensionDef, arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("param2"));

			if ($val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].ibcrYellowFace') == 'true') {
				
				
				targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('140',
					$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
					arguments[0], arguments[2],
					$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
					$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),
					"IbcrMadoguchi"));
				
			} else {
				
				
				targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('140', 
					$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
					arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			}
			
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("assginCauseAnalysisNo"));
			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('135', 
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),

				$val('syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargePersonCode'), $val('syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1ChargeDeptCode'),
				"150"));
			
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_A:
			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("assginCauseAnalysisNo"));
			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('140',
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),

				"IbcrMadoguchi",
				arguments[1]));

			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('141',
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonEndViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonEndViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonEndViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),

				"IbcrMadoguchi",
				arguments[1]));

			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('142',
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00DecisionChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00DecisionChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00DecisionChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),

				"IbcrMadoguchi",
				arguments[1]));

			
			
			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('143',
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqaa170ApprOneECNViewEntity.userCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqaa170ApprOneECNViewEntity.deptCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00IbcrMadoguchiViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),

				"IbcrMadoguchi",
				arguments[1]));

			
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('144',
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCodeExtension'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqaa170ApprOneActionViewEntity.userCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqaa170ApprOneActionViewEntity.deptCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode'),
				$val('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + idx + '].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode'),

				"135",
				arguments[1]));

			
			break;

		
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('150', extensionDef,
				arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			
			break;
		
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('330', extensionDef,
				arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			
			break;
			

		
		case MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			targetList.push(getReplaceInfo('520', extensionDef,
				arguments[0], arguments[2]));
			
			break;

			
		default:
			break;
	}
	
	
	var returnValue = 0;
	
	
	claerSYQAA090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityHidden();
	
	
	var j = 0;		
	for (var i = 0; i < targetList.length; i++) {
		
		var currentUserId = targetList[i].currentUserId;
		var currentDeptCode = targetList[i].currentDeptCode;
		
		if (currentUserId == '' && currentDeptCode == '') {
			continue;
		}
		
		
		var effective = isEffectiveDeptOrUser(currentUserId, currentDeptCode);
		
		if (effective != '1') {
			
			var newProcessCode	= targetList[i].newProcess;
			var newUserId		= targetList[i].newUserId;
			var newDeptCode		= targetList[i].newDeptCode;
			var newBaseCode		= "";
			
			
			
			if ("INA28423" != newUserId
				&& "IbcrMadoguchi" != newProcessCode 
				&& "1" != isEffectiveDeptOrUser(newUserId, newDeptCode)) {
				
				newUserId   = "INA28423";
				newDeptCode = "1400553";
				newBaseCode = "14";
			} else {
				
				if ("INA28423" == newUserId) {
					
					newBaseCode = "14";
				} else {
					newBaseCode = getDeptUserBaseCode(newDeptCode);
				}
			}
			
			var currentBaseCode = getDeptUserBaseCode(currentDeptCode);
			if ('' == currentBaseCode) {
				currentBaseCode = currentDeptCode.substring(0,2);
			}
			
			if (currentBaseCode != newBaseCode) {
				
				if (currentBaseCode != "14") {
					
					return -1;
				} else {
					
					newUserId   = "INA28423";
					newDeptCode = "1400553";
					newBaseCode = "14";
				}
			}
			
			
			if ("INA28423" == newUserId) {

				
				if (targetList[i].mstKbn != MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_A
					|| targetList[i].targetProcess == '140') {
					returnValue = 1;
				} else {
					
					targetList[i].addMemoCommentFlg = "1";
					targetList[i].newProcess = '137';
				}

			}
			
			
			targetList[i].newUserId = newUserId;
			targetList[i].newDeptCode = newDeptCode;
			
			targetList[i].replaceFlg = "1";
		}
		
		
		addSYQAA090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityHidden(targetList[i], j);
		j++;
	}


	
	replaceCount = j;


	return returnValue;
}


function checkCompleteCancelMain() {
	

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	var effectiveResult = changeUnEffectiveChargeDeptOrPerson(arguments[0], arguments[1], arguments[2]);
	
	
	if (effectiveResult < 0) {
		
		alert('YQ10283E: Cannot proceed with this transaction \n because the person of this job process currently, has been deleted, has no authority: Editing Documents or has moved to another base or another Dgt. \n Let a person from the same Dgt as the person of this cancel this or Contact your system administrator.');
		document.body.style.cursor = 'auto';
		return false;
	} else if (0 < effectiveResult) {
		
		if (!comConfirm('YQ10301W: The person of this job process currently, has been deleted, \n has no authority: Editing Documents or has moved to another base or another Dgt. \n Will you replace him/ her by yourself?')) {
			return false;
		}
	}
	
	return true;
}



function checkFinalConfirmCompleteCancel() {
	
	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}
	
	if (MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE_CANCEL == arguments[0]

		&& 'L00209' == 'L00209'
		&& '1400553' != '1400566'
		&& 'false' == 'false') {

		case CONFIRM_OK:
				break;
			case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
				return false;
			default:
				break;
		}
	}
	

		
	if (MST_KBN_TSR_COMPLETE_CANCEL == arguments[0]
		&& '1400553' != ''
		&& 'false' == 'false') {

		switch (comConfirmOkCancel('YQ10346W: The reply to the distributor has to be effected with this cancel of the completion.\n\nAre you absolutely sure to do so?', 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION + VB_DEFAULT_BUTTON2)) {
		
		case CONFIRM_OK:
				break;
			case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
				return false;
			default:
				break;
		}
	}


	return true;
}




function checkCompleteCancel() {
	

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	var isApprovePowerCancel = false;
	
	switch (arguments[1].charAt(0)) {
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("syqu00ApAPICEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value != "") {
				isApprovePowerCancel = true;
			}
			break;
		case MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("param2"));
			if (document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + idx + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value != "") {
				isApprovePowerCancel = true;
			}
			break;
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("param2"));
			if (document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + idx + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value != "") {
				isApprovePowerCancel = true;
			}
			break;
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjApprovalPICEntityList.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value != "") {
				isApprovePowerCancel = true;
			}
			break;
		case MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			if (document.getElementsByName("feedbackApproveChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value != "") {
				isApprovePowerCancel = true;
			}
			break;
		default:
			break;
	}
	
	if (isApprovePowerCancel) {
		
		if (!comConfirm('YQ10373W: With this operation, the approval of the job process will be automatically cancelled. Are you sure to go with that?')) {
			return false;
		}
	} else {
		
		if (!comConfirm('YQ10076W: Executes the cancel. Are you sure to go?')) {
			return false;
		}
	}


	
	if (!checkFinalConfirmCompleteCancel(arguments[1])) {
		return false;
	}



	
	if (MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE_CANCEL == arguments[1]
		&& '2' == '1'
		&& '1' == isBeforeCmeasureDecided('IN202310B06811')) {


		if (false) {

			switch (comConfirmOkCancel('YQ10411W: The process of SUMMARY OF C\'MEASURE COURSES has been completed.\r\nAnd then, even if the supersession of FTIR is canceled, the FTIR will appear in the list of Incident Vehicles in FPCR.\r\nThe shaded FTIRs in the list mean their supersessions were canceled. Also, to remove those shaded FTIRs from the list, you have to cancel the completion of \"Summary of C\'measure Courses\". Do you cancel the approval of \"ACTION JUDGEMENT\"?', 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {

				case CONFIRM_OK:
					break;
				case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
					return false;
				default:
					break;
			}
		}

	}


	
	if (!checkCompleteCancelMain(arguments[0], arguments[1], arguments[2])) {
		return false;
	}
	

	
	switch (arguments[1].charAt(0)) {
		case MST_KBN_JUDGEMENT_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			
			if (replaceCount > 0) {
				
				var replaceFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].replaceFlg")[0].value;
				if (replaceFlg == "1") {
					var changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].changeUserId")[0].value;
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(changeUserId, true)) {
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}
			}


			
			if(document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1WaitingFlg")[0].value == "1"
					&& document.getElementById("AJDATA2").value == ""){
				document.getElementsByName("syqu00TjPICEntityList.g1WaitingFlg")[0].value = "0";
			}

			
			break;
		case MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			

			var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("param2"));
			var changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + idx + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;

			
			if (replaceCount > 0) {
				
				var replaceFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].replaceFlg")[0].value;
				if (replaceFlg == "1") {
					changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].changeUserId")[0].value;
				}
			}

			if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4InvestigationAssign(changeUserId, idx, true)) {
				document.body.style.cursor = "default";
				return false;
			}

			break;
		case MST_KBN_ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE_CANCEL:
			
			

			
			isSelectedOkAtCraDeleteDialog = false;

			for (var i = 0; i < replaceCount; i++) {
				var replaceFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[" + i + "].replaceFlg")[0].value;
				if (replaceFlg != "1") {
					continue;
				}
				
				var targetProcessCode = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[" + i + "].targetProcessCode")[0].value;
				if (targetProcessCode != "150"
					 && targetProcessCode != "140") {
					continue;
				}

				var changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[" + i + "].changeUserId")[0].value;

				
				if (targetProcessCode == "150") {
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4AjAssign(changeUserId, true)) {
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}

				
				if (isSelectedOkAtCraDeleteDialog) {
					break;
				}

				
				if (targetProcessCode == "140") {
					var targetProcessCodeExtension = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[" + i + "].targetProcessCodeExtension")[0].value; 
					var idx = rcNoTolistIndex("RC" + targetProcessCodeExtension);
					if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4InvestigationAssign(changeUserId, idx, false)) {
						document.body.style.cursor = "default";
						return false;
					}
				}

				
				if (isSelectedOkAtCraDeleteDialog) {
					break;
				}

			}

			break;
		default:
			break;
	}




	return true;
}

function checkRepeatedComplete() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}




	
	if (!checkFinalConfirmCompleteCancel(arguments[2])) {
		return false;
	}

function dispOrHideFPCR(value) {
	if (value == 1
			|| value == 3) {
		dispFPCR(value);
	} else if (value == 2
			|| value == 4) {
		hideFPCR(value);
	} else {
		hideFPCR(value);
	}
}
function dispFPCR(value) {
	
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value = value;

	document.getElementById("FpcrTheme").style.display = "";

	buttonTjKanryo(value);


	dispQuickBehold(value);



	controlAJCheckboxDisplay();

}
function hideFPCR(value) {
	document.getElementById("FpcrTheme").style.display = "none";

	
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value = value;

	buttonTjKanryo(value);



	controlAJCheckboxDisplay();

}

		return;

	if (infoStart && !infoComplete && !aJComplete) {

		if (value == 2) {
			
			document.getElementById("syqaa090Form_buttonControltjKanryo").disabled = false;
		} else {
			
			document.getElementById("syqaa090Form_buttonControltjKanryo").disabled = true;
		}
	}

	
	if (infoComplete && !investigationAllCompleteCheck('ibcrAppr')) {
		if (value == 2) {
			
			document.getElementById("syqaa090Form_buttonControltjKanryo").disabled = false;
		} else {
			
			document.getElementById("syqaa090Form_buttonControltjKanryo").disabled = true;
		}
	}

}




function AlertMsgCtrl(value,radiovalue) {
	if (value=="0") {
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_dispFlg").value = "0";
		document.getElementById("AlertMsgSpan").style.display =  "none";
		


		document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdFtir').style.display     = "none";
		document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdWarranty').style.display = "none";
		document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdVehicle').style.display = "none";
		
		
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertFtir')[0].value     = "false";
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertWarranty')[0].value = "false";
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertVehicle')[0].value  = "false";

		
		return;
	}
	
	if (document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_isCompleteFlg").value == "1" 
				&& document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession')[0].value.trim()) {
	
		if (value=="2" && radiovalue!="2") {
			return;
		}
	

		// 閾値
		var figure = document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_figure").value;
		
		// 関連FTIR件数

		getFtirs(document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession')[0].value);
		var ftirCnt = parseInt(document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeFtirs')[0].value);
		var vehicleCnt   = parseInt(document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeVehicles')[0].value);
		

		var reportVehiclesValue = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.strReportVehicles')[0].value;

		
		
		if (!integerCheck(reportVehiclesValue)) {
			reportVehiclesValue = 0;
		}
		
		ftirCnt = ftirCnt + 1;
		vehicleCnt   = vehicleCnt   + Number(reportVehiclesValue);
		


		document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_g1Ftirs").value = ftirCnt;

		document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_g1Vehicles").value = vehicleCnt;

		if (vehicleCnt >= figure) {
			document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_dispFlg").value = "1";

			document.getElementById("AlertMsgTd").innerText = 
				document.getElementById("AlertMsgReplace").innerText.replace("AlertMsgReplaceFtirs", 
																			 callSYQU00ServiceImpGetDispFtirCountMain(ftirCnt, vehicleCnt));




			document.getElementById("AlertMsgSpan").style.display =  "";
		} else {
			document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_dispFlg").value = "0";
			document.getElementById("AlertMsgSpan").style.display =  "none";
		}
		

		
		
		warrantyToAlertNotifyFromSYQAA050Button();
		
		checkWarrantyToWatchAlertNotify();

	}


	var chkSupersedeFtirs = parseInt(document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeFtirs')[0].value);
	if (chkSupersedeFtirs <= 0) {
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090FtirsViewEntity_dispFlg").value = "0";
		document.getElementById("AlertMsgSpan").style.display =  "none";
		document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SbIdSupersessionDisplay').style.backgroundColor = "red";
	}


}




function warrantyToAlertNotifyFromSYQAA050Button() {


	var reportVehiclesValue = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.strReportVehicles')[0].value;

	
	
	if (!integerCheck(reportVehiclesValue)) {
		reportVehiclesValue = 0;
	}

		
	//-- 半角英数しか渡さない為、エンコードは不要と思われる --//
	var retVal;
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: false,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,

		parameters: "key=" + document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SbIdSupersessionDisplay').innerText
				  + "&key2=" + reportVehiclesValue , 

		onComplete: function(request) {
			if (request == null || request.responseText == "null") {
				retVal = '';
			} else {
				retVal = request.responseText;
			}
		}
		
	}


	document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1SbIdSupersessionDisplay').style.backgroundColor = "";


	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EW1SFnd.do", ajaxOptions);
	
	if (retVal != "") {
	
		var warrantyToWatchAlertNotifyArray = eval("(" + retVal + ")"); // 戻り値が連想配列に置き換わる
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertFtir')[0].value     = warrantyToWatchAlertNotifyArray.alertFtir;
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertWarranty')[0].value = warrantyToWatchAlertNotifyArray.alertWarranty;

		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertVehicle')[0].value = warrantyToWatchAlertNotifyArray.alertVehicle;

	}
}




function callSYQU00ServiceImpGetDispFtirCountMain(allSupersedeFtirs, allReportVehicles) {

	//-- 半角英数しか渡さない為、エンコードは不要と思われる --//
	var retVal;
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: false,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,
		parameters:  "key="  + allSupersedeFtirs
				  + "&key2=" + allReportVehicles , 
		onComplete: function(request) {
			if (request == null || request.responseText == "null") {
				retVal = '';
			} else {
				retVal = request.responseText;
			}
		}
	}
	
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EW2SFnd.do", ajaxOptions);
	
	if (retVal != "") {
		return eval("(" + retVal + ")");
	}
}



function getFtirs(sbId) {
	
	var retVal;
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: false,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,
		parameters: "key=" + sbId,
		onComplete: function(request) {
			if (request == null) {
				retVal = '';
			}
			retVal = request.responseText;
		}
	}
	
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/getFtirsOfSb.do", ajaxOptions);
	

	if (retVal != "") {
	
		var supersedeCountArray = eval("(" + retVal + ")"); // 戻り値が連想配列に置き換わる
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeFtirs')[0].value    = supersedeCountArray.g1SupersedeFtirs;
		
		document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeVehicles')[0].value = supersedeCountArray.g1SupersedeVehicles;
	}

}




function openInputSupportWindow(prcsKbn, userId, deptCd) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var retVal = new Array();

	var bnriCd = "";
	var inputSupportActionPath = "";
	
	if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_R
		|| prcsKbn == MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_A) {
		
		bnriCd = MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_COMPLETE_CANCEL;

	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_R
		|| prcsKbn == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_A) {
		
		bnriCd = MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL;

	} else {
		
		bnriCd = MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_COMPLETE_CANCEL;
	}
	
	
	if (!checkCompleteCancelMain(userId, bnriCd, deptCd)) {
		return false;
	}



	if (bnriCd == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_COMPLETE_CANCEL) {
		
		

		var idx = rcNoTolistIndex($val("param2"));
		var changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + idx + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;

		
		if (replaceCount > 0) {

			
			var replaceFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].replaceFlg")[0].value;
			if (replaceFlg == "1") {
				changeUserId = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ChargePersonReplaceViewEntityList[0].changeUserId")[0].value;
			}
		}

		if (!investigationCraDeleteCheckAndDialog4InvestigationAssign(changeUserId, idx, true)) {
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;
		}
	}



	if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_R) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EC3SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EC3SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		// /SYQAA090EC3SUpd"
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EC3SUpd.do";
	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_FEEDBACK_A) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EC5SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EC5SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		// /SYQAA090EC5SUpd
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EC5SUpd.do";

	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_R) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EL7SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EL7SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		// /SYQAA090EL7SUpd
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EL7SUpd.do";
	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_IBCR_RAISE_A) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EM1SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EM1SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		// /SYQAA090EM1SUpd
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EM1SUpd.do";


	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_R) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EL7SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EL7SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EN7SUpd.do";
		// /SYQAA090EN7SUpd
	} else if (prcsKbn == MST_KBN_CAUSE_INVESTIGATION_A) {
		
		//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EM1SUpd','','','1008','710');
		comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA090EM1SUpd','','openInputSupportWindow','1008','710');
		// /SYQAA090EO1SUpd
		inputSupportActionPath = "/SYQAA090EO1SUpd.do";


	} else {
		
		document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = "";
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}
	
	document.getElementById('inputSupportActionPath').value = inputSupportActionPath;
	
	return false;
	
}



function openInputSupportWindowReturn(){
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	

	
	for(var i = 0; i < retVal.length; i++) {
		if(retVal[i]) {
			retVal[i] = retVal[i].replace("&#44;", ",");
		}
	}


	
	if (typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}
	
	saveButtonInfoPopUpParam2(retVal[2]);




	// 各差戻アクションの呼び出しを行う
	var inputSupportActionPath = document.getElementById('inputSupportActionPath').value;
	
	if (inputSupportActionPath != "") {
		document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + inputSupportActionPath;
		document.forms[0].submit();
	}


	return true;
}



function saveButtonInfoPopUpParam2(param2Val) {
	document.getElementsByName("param2")[0].value = param2Val;
}

function cancellation_Syonin() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	

	if (!comConfirm('YQ10119W: This FTIR becomes in waiting for Approval when you cancel the Approval. \n Redo the Approval/rejection judgement, finding it in Approval field of your Job List.\n\nClick on \"-> Go to The CHECK & APPROVE VIEW\", when you want to open CHECK & APPROVE view promptly.')) {
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;

	}


	
	if ('2' == '1'
		&& '1' == isBeforeCmeasureDecided('IN202310B06811')) {


		if (false) {

			switch (comConfirmOkCancel('YQ10411W: The process of SUMMARY OF C\'MEASURE COURSES has been completed.\r\nAnd then, even if the supersession of FTIR is canceled, the FTIR will appear in the list of Incident Vehicles in FPCR.\r\nThe shaded FTIRs in the list mean their supersessions were canceled. Also, to remove those shaded FTIRs from the list, you have to cancel the completion of \"Summary of C\'measure Courses\". Do you cancel the approval of \"ACTION JUDGEMENT\"?', 'EXCLAMATION', VB_ICON_EXCLAMATION)) {

				case CONFIRM_OK:
					break;
				case CONFIRM_CANCEL:
					document.body.style.cursor = "default";
					return false;
				default:
					break;
			}
		}

	}



	return true;

}

function pendingDateCalculate(baseDate, idxTarget) {
	if (baseDate == '') {
		return;
	}

	if ($("hdnPendDate_" + idxTarget).value.trim() != '') {
		return;
	}
	
	var currentYear  = parseInt(baseDate.substr(0, 4), 10);

	var currentMonth = parseInt(baseDate.substr(4, 2), 10) - 1;

	var currentDate  = parseInt(baseDate.substr(6, 2), 10);
	
	var newDate = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, currentDate  + 20);

	var year = newDate.getFullYear();

	var month = newDate.getMonth() + 1;

	var date = newDate.getDate();

	
	month = ('0' + month).slice(-2);
	date = ('0' + date).slice(-2);
	
	var displayValue = '';
	var hiddenValue  = '' + year + month + date;
	
	if ('ja_JP' == 'en_US') {
		
		displayValue = '' + year + '.' + month + '.' + date;
	} else {
		
		
		var monthList = new Array(12);
		monthList[0]  = "Jan";
		monthList[1]  = "Feb";
		monthList[2]  = "Mar";
		monthList[3]  = "Apr";
		monthList[4]  = "May";
		monthList[5]  = "Jun";
		monthList[6]  = "Jul";
		monthList[7]  = "Aug";
		monthList[8]  = "Sep";
		monthList[9]  = "Oct";
		monthList[10] = "Nov";
		monthList[11] = "Dec";
		
		displayValue = date + '.' + monthList[parseInt(month - 1, 10)] + "." + year;
	}
	
	$("pendDate_"  + idxTarget).innerText = displayValue; 
	$("hdnPendDate_" + idxTarget).value = hiddenValue;

}




function controlDdlRankLevelSecondaryJudgement() {


	var secondaryFlg = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1RankSecondaryJudgement")[0].value;// 重要度セカンダリープルダウンの選択値
	var rankLevelDdl = document.getElementById("ddlRankLevelCodeSecondaryJudgement");// 重要度レベル(二次)DDL本体
	var rankLevelHdn = document.getElementById("hdnRankLevelCodeSecondaryJudgement");// 重要度レベル(二次)DDLの値をサーバーに飛ばすためのhidden
	
	
	if (rankLevelDdl) {


		if (secondaryFlg != "") {
			
			
			rankLevelDdl.disabled = false;

		} else {
			
			
			for (var i = 0; i < rankLevelDdl.options.length; i++) {
				if (rankLevelDdl.options[i].value == "") {
					rankLevelDdl.options[i].selected = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			rankLevelDdl.disabled = true;
			rankLevelHdn.value= "";
		}
	}
}


function changeDdlRankLevelSecondaryJudgement() {


	
	var rankLevelDdl = document.getElementById("ddlRankLevelCodeSecondaryJudgement");
	var rankLevelHdn = document.getElementById("hdnRankLevelCodeSecondaryJudgement");
	rankLevelHdn.value = rankLevelDdl.value;

}


function fpcrDeliberationFlgControl() {

	if ($('fpcrDeliberationFlg').checked) {
		if ($("fpcrDeliberationFlgHidden")) {
			$("fpcrDeliberationFlgHidden").value = "1";
		}
 
		$('fpcrPendingFlg').disabled = false;
		siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrPendingFlg'));
 
	} else {
		if ($("fpcrDeliberationFlgHidden")) {
			$("fpcrDeliberationFlgHidden").value = "0";
		}
 
		
		$('fpcrPendingFlg').checked = false;
		siftCtrl.changeCheck($('fpcrPendingFlg'));
		$('fpcrPendingFlg').disabled = true;
		siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrPendingFlg'));
		
		
		if ($('fpcrPendingFlgHidden')) {
			$('fpcrPendingFlgHidden').value = "0";
		}
 
	}
}


function onChangeCalendar() {
	if (arguments[0].value.trim() == "") {



		document.getElementById(arguments[1]).checked = false;
	} else {
		document.getElementById(arguments[1]).checked = true;
	}

	if (null != arguments[2]) {
		showRequiredMark(arguments[1], arguments[2], null, null, arguments[3]);
	}

}



function onChangeCalendarOfSubPerson() {
	if (arguments[0].value.trim() == "") {
		// ｶﾚﾝﾀﾞｰの日付が無い場合
		document.getElementById(arguments[1]).checked = false;
	} else {
		// ｶﾚﾝﾀﾞｰの日付が有る場合
		document.getElementById(arguments[1]).checked = true;
	}
}



function showRequiredMark() {
	//
	if (document.getElementById(arguments[0]).checked) {
		if (null != arguments[4] && document.getElementById(arguments[4]).value == "0") {
			document.getElementById(arguments[1]).style.visibility = "hidden";
		} else {
			document.getElementById(arguments[1]).style.visibility = "visible";
		}
	} else {
		document.getElementById(arguments[1]).style.visibility = "hidden";
		//
		if (null != arguments[2]) {
			setValue(arguments[2], "");
			if (null != arguments[3]) {
				setValue(arguments[3], "");
			}
		}
	}
}




function onChangeWaitingFlg( element, dataId, dataHeddenId, langCode ) {


	// ﾁｪｯｸ判定
	if ( element.checked != true ) {
		// ﾁｪｯｸなし
		document.getElementById(dataId).innerHTML     = '';
		document.getElementById(dataHeddenId).value = '';
		return;
	}

	// 本日、１週間後の日付編集
	var nowNextDate = new Date(); 
	nowNextDate.setDate(nowNextDate.getDate() + 7);


	// 日付フォーマット編集
	var waitingDate = "";
	if (langCode && langCode.length >= 2
		&& (langCode.substring(0, 2) == "ja"
			|| langCode.substring(0, 2) == "zh")) { // 国コードも含む形式(ja-JP)での指定も考慮が必要
		// 日本語・中国語形式の場合、"YYYY.MM.DD"形式
		waitingDate = 'YYYY.MM.DD';
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/YYYY/g, nowNextDate.getFullYear());
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/MM/g, ('0' + (nowNextDate.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2));
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/DD/g, ('0' + nowNextDate.getDate()).slice(-2));
	} else {
		// 日本語・中国語形式以外の場合
		waitingDate = nowNextDate.getDate() + "." + monthEng[parseInt(nowNextDate.getMonth(), 10)] + "." + nowNextDate.getFullYear();
	}


	// 該当項目に設定
	document.getElementById(dataId).innerHTML   = waitingDate;
	document.getElementById(dataHeddenId).value = 
		nowNextDate.getFullYear() 
			+ ('0' + (nowNextDate.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) 
			+ ('0' + nowNextDate.getDate()).slice(-2);

}

function saveButtonInfoForReportChargeDept(idxBase, baseCode, idxReportCharge) {
	
	var selectedRowNo = '';
	var elems = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090MeetingViewEntity[' + idxBase + '].selectedReportChargeDeptIndex');
	for (var i = 0; i < elems.length; i++) {
		if (elems[i].checked) {
			selectedRowNo = elems[i].value;
			break;
		}
	}
	
	
	document.getElementsByName("deleteReportBaseCd")[0].value = baseCode;
	document.getElementsByName("deleteReportRecNo")[0].value = selectedRowNo;

	
	document.getElementsByName("selectReportRecNo")[0].value = idxReportCharge;
}


function onCloseSYQAA090() {
	

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	if (comCheckInputChange()) {

		var btnClose = "L00494";
		var btnSave = "L00239";
		var btnCancel = "L00495";
		var paramAry = {};
		paramAry["messageId"] = "YQ10165I";
		paramAry["buttonId"] = btnClose + "," + btnSave + "," + btnCancel;

		
		var returnAry = [];
		returnAry.push(btnClose, btnSave);
	
		document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();

		//var ret = comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, '', 460, 150);
		comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, 'onCloseSYQAA090', 460, 150);
		
		return false;
	
		
	} else {
		

		inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";

		comCloseCheckInput();
	}
}



function onCloseSYQAA090Return(){
	var ret;
	var btnClose;
	var btnSave;
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
  	
	
	btnClose = returnAry[0];
	btnSave = returnAry[1];
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		ret = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	if (btnClose == ret) {
		
		inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";
		comCloseCheckInput();
	} else if (btnSave == ret) {
		
		document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value='1';

		if (comSubmit("1")) {
			document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EB9SFnd.do";
			document.forms[0].submit();
		}

	} else {
		
		return false;
	}
}



function btnClearCollectionRequestClick() {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}

	


	
	let confirmMsg = 'YQ10490W: Are you sure to cancel the collection request?';
	
	
	let btnClearCollectionWish = document.getElementById('btnClearCollectionWish');
	if ( btnClearCollectionWish != null && btnClearCollectionWish.disabled != true ){
		confirmMsg = 'YQ10252W: Are you sure to cancel the collection request, in spite of at least a wisher existing?';
	}
	
	
	if (!comConfirm(confirmMsg)) {
		return false;
	}

	// 回収要求処理コードをセット
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionProcessCode").value = '2';




	return true;

}

	}

	



	return true;

}

function checkCompleteWithoutFlg(procCd) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	var actionPath = "";
	var ctrlId = "";
	
	
	if (procCd == "110") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090E38SUpd.do";
		ctrlId = "apWithoutApprovalFlg";
	} else if (procCd == "150") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090E92SUpd.do";
		ctrlId = "tjWithoutApprovalFlg";
	} else if (procCd == "135") {

		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090EM4SUpd.do";
		ctrlId = "";

	} else if (procCd == "330") {
		
		actionPath = "/SYQAA090EB1SUpd.do";
		ctrlId = "fbWithoutApprovalFlg";
	}
	
	var btnSkip = "L00580";
	var btnNotSkip = "L00582";
	var paramAry = {};
	paramAry["messageId"] = "YQ10266W";
	paramAry["buttonId"] = btnSkip + "," + btnNotSkip;

	
	var returnAry = [];
	returnAry.push(actionPath, ctrlId);
	
	document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();
	
	//var ret = comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, '', 480, 150);
	comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, 'checkCompleteWithoutFlg', 480, 150);
		
	return false;
	

}



function checkCompleteWithoutFlgReturn(){
	var ret;
	var actionPath = "";
	var ctrlId = "";
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
  	
	
	actionPath = returnAry[0];
	ctrlId = returnAry[1];
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		ret = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	
	var btnSkip = "L00580";
	var btnNotSkip = "L00582";
	
	if (btnSkip == ret) {
		

		if (ctrlId) {
			$(ctrlId).checked = true;
		}

		

		if (comSubmit("1")) {
			inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";
			document.body.style.cursor = 'wait';
			document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + actionPath;
			document.forms[0].submit();
		}

	} else if (btnNotSkip == ret) {
		

		if (ctrlId) {
			$(ctrlId).checked = false;
		}

		

		if (comSubmit("1")) {
			inputCancelMsgFlg = "0";
			document.body.style.cursor = 'wait';
			document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + actionPath;
			document.forms[0].submit();
		}

	} else {
		
		return;
	}
}




function personLinkDispatch(obj, processCode, firRowSpan, finAssignProc, proccessCompleteDate, personCode, deptCode
							, mailProcessCode, mailprocessCodeExtension, mailPlaceOfUserId
							, openCloseIconName, openCloseJudgmentId) {


	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	
	var isMailSend = false;

	
	if (!proccessCompleteDate || !proccessCompleteDate.trim()) {
		isMailSend = true;
	}
	
	
	if (personCode.trim() || deptCode.trim()) {
		isMailSend = true;
	}

	if (isMailSend) {
		
		execAjaxMail(mailProcessCode, mailprocessCodeExtension, mailPlaceOfUserId);
	} else {
		dispFoldControl(openCloseJudgmentId, openCloseIconName, true);
		
		
		procPersonDisplay(getParentByTagName(obj, "TABLE"), processCode, firRowSpan, finAssignProc);
		
	}

}



function controlForSpecialAffairsBlock() {

	
	
	if (document.getElementsByName("syqaa090ItemControlViewEntity.disabledFlgSpecialAffairs")[0].value == "false") {

		var confirmTextEn = document.getElementById("SpecialAffairsEn_ConfirmText");
		var editTextEn = document.getElementById("SpecialAffairsEn_EditText");
		var confirmTextPl = document.getElementById("SpecialAffairsPl_ConfirmText");
		var editTextPl = document.getElementById("SpecialAffairsPl_EditText");

		if (editStatus == "1") {
			
			editButton.disabled = false;
			confirmButton.disabled = true;
			cancelButton.disabled = true;

			confirmTextEn.style.display = "";
			editTextEn.style.display = "none";
			confirmTextPl.style.display = "";
			editTextPl.style.display = "none";
		} else if (editStatus == "2") {
			
			editButton.disabled = true;
			confirmButton.disabled = false;
			cancelButton.disabled = false;

// TODO
			confirmTextEn.style.display = "none";
			editTextEn.style.display = "";
			editTextEn.style.overflow = "auto";
			confirmTextPl.style.display = "none";
			editTextPl.style.display = "";
			stretchTextArea();
			
		} else if (editStatus == "3") {
			
			editButton.disabled = false;
			confirmButton.disabled = true;
			cancelButton.disabled = false;

			confirmTextEn.style.display = "";
			editTextEn.style.display = "none";
			confirmTextPl.style.display = "";
			editTextPl.style.display = "none";
		}
	}

}
