function openArea(id) {

	// switchDisplayのID
	var element = document.getElementById(id);
	// switchDisplayの展開LINKのID
	var elementLink = document.getElementById(id + 'Link');
	
	if (element.style.display == "none") {
		// 展開LINKを強制クリックする。
		elementLink.click();
	}

	
	document.body.style.display = 'none';
	document.body.style.display = '';
}



function checkSave() {

	var nowBaseCode = document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseCode').value; 
	var initBaseCode = document.getElementById('initBaseCode_ID').value; 
	if (nowBaseCode != initBaseCode) {
		
		return comConfirm('YQ10325W: Transaction Base of this FTIR shall be the one that the user re-assigned to as the person of RECEIVING belongs to. \n With this operation, all the data except that by the FTIR reporter shall be deleted. Are you sure to go?');
	}
	return true;
}



function checkDeliberation(rcNo, pCd) {

	if ("137" != pCd
		&& "999" != pCd) {
		return true;
	}

	
	var causeInfoList = new Array();
	
	
		
		
		
			causeInfoList.push({causeAnalysisNo : 'RC01'					
								, causeAnalysisBaseCode : '14'}		
								);
							
		
	

	var deliberationList = new Array();
	
		if ("" != ''
			 || "" != '') {
			 
			deliberationList.push({conductionBaseCode : '14'});
		 }
	

	var baseCode = "";
	for (var i = 0; i < causeInfoList.length; i++) {
		
		if (rcNo == causeInfoList[i].causeAnalysisNo) {
			baseCode = causeInfoList[i].causeAnalysisBaseCode;
		}
	}

	var baseCnt = 0;
	for (var i = 0; i < causeInfoList.length; i++) {
		
		if (baseCode == causeInfoList[i].causeAnalysisBaseCode) {
			baseCnt++;
		}
	}

	if (1 == baseCnt) {
		
		for (var i = 0; i < deliberationList.length; i++) {
			if (baseCode == deliberationList[i].conductionBaseCode) {
				alert('Cannot proceed in this transaction, because the meeting minutes have been existing.');
				document.body.style.cursor = 'auto';
				return false;
			}
		}
	}
	return true;

}



function onChangeIsTemp(workPath) {

	document.getElementsByName(workPath + '.isTemp')[0].value = '1'
}




function vehicleInformationSearch() {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}
	
	
	
	var vin = "MFAHFJjhg123GD34J2352";
	if (vin == "" || vin == null) {
		alert('No information from the VIN');
		return false;
	}
	
	
	
	var vehicleInformationJson = getFtirVinSearch(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1Vin")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductCategory")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.f1ReportCountryCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductModelCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SalesModelCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Fcok")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1DestinationCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureCountryCode")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Abok")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoEgNo")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoTransNo")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoBodyColor")[0].value,
												  
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoDrive")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoGearbox")[0].value,
												  document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Shipmentday")[0].value

												  );
		

	if (vehicleInformationJson != "") {
		var vehicleInformation = eval("(" + vehicleInformationJson + ")");
		
		
		if (vehicleInformation.returnCode == '100') {
			if (comConfirm(vehicleInformation.updateMessage)) {
				
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ProductModelCode")[0].value = vehicleInformation.xy;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SalesModelCode")[0].value = vehicleInformation.salesModelCode;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Fcok")[0].value = vehicleInformation.fcok;
				$("Fcok1").innerText = vehicleInformation.fcokView;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1DestinationCode")[0].value = vehicleInformation.destinationCode;
				$("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_f1DestinationView").innerText = vehicleInformation.destinationView;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureFactoryCode")[0].value = vehicleInformation.manufactureFactoryCode;
				$("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_f1ManufactureFactoryView").innerText = vehicleInformation.manufactureFactoryView;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ManufactureCountryCode")[0].value = vehicleInformation.manufactureFactoryCountryCode;
				$("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_f1ManufactureFactoryCountryView").innerText = vehicleInformation.manufactureFactoryCountryView;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Abok")[0].value = vehicleInformation.abok;
				$("editTextFlagCarBlock1").innerText = vehicleInformation.abokView;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoEgNo")[0].value = vehicleInformation.engineNo;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoTransNo")[0].value = vehicleInformation.transNo;
				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoBodyColor")[0].value = vehicleInformation.bodyColor;

				document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1Shipmentday")[0].value = vehicleInformation.syukkaUpdate;
				$("Shipmentday1").innerText = vehicleInformation.syukkaUpdateView;
				

				
				
				var driveList = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoDrive')[0].options;
				for (var i = 0; i < driveList.length ; i++) {
					if (driveList[i].value == vehicleInformation.driveCode) {
						driveList[i].selected = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				var gearboxList = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1InfoGearbox')[0].options;

				for (var i = 0; i < gearboxList.length ; i++) {
					if (gearboxList[i].value == vehicleInformation.gearboxCode) {
						gearboxList[i].selected = true;
						break;
					}
				}

				
				updateCheckInputChange("true");
			}
		}else{
				alert(vehicleInformation.updateMessage);
		}
	}else{
		alert('No information from the VIN');
	}
}




function investigationAllCompleteCheck(value) {


	
	var infoComplete = document.getElementsByName("syqu00infoChargePersonNameViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

	if (!infoComplete) {
		return false;
	}

	
	 
	var causeAnalysisCnt = 1;

	for (var i = 0; i < causeAnalysisCnt; i++) {

		
		var dataKbnElements = document.getElementsByName('causeAnalysisViewEntity[' + i + '].dataKbn');

		
		if (!dataKbnElements) {
			continue;
		}

		
		if (dataKbnElements[0].value == '3') {
			continue;
		}

		
		var ibcrRaiseDept = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;
		var ibcrRaiseComplete = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].syqu00ChargePersonIbcrKianViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;
		
		var ibcrRaiseApprDept = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;
		var ibcrRaiseApprComplete = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].ibcrKianApproveEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

		
		var invRaiseDept = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;
		var invComplete = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationChargePersonViewEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;
		
		var invApprDept = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value;
		var invApprComplete = document.getElementsByName("causeAnalysisViewEntity[" + i + "].investigationApproveEntity.g1ProccessCompleteDate")[0].value;

		if (value) {
			
			if (ibcrRaiseDept && ibcrRaiseComplete
				&& ibcrRaiseApprDept && !ibcrRaiseApprComplete) {
				return false;
			}
		} else {
			
			if (ibcrRaiseDept && !ibcrRaiseComplete) {
					return false;
			} else if (ibcrRaiseApprDept && !ibcrRaiseApprComplete) {
				return false;
			}
	
			
			if (invRaiseDept && !invComplete) {
				return false;
			} else if (invApprDept && !invApprComplete) {
				return false;
			}
		}
	}

	return true;
}

	// 引数 ﾃﾞﾌｫﾙﾄ(空文字)
	idx = idx || "";

	document.getElementsByName("pushSubPersonProccessCode")[0].value = cd;
	document.getElementsByName("pushSubPersonCauseAnalysisIndex")[0].value = idx;

}




function initControlForMeetingMemoBlock() {
	
	 
	var meetingMemoCnt = 0;

	for (var i = 0; i < meetingMemoCnt; i++) {
		
		controlForMeetingMemoBlock(i);
	}
}

function controlForMeetingMemoBlock(i) {

	
	var memoBlock = document.getElementById("meetingMemoResult_" + i);
	memoBlock.style.display = "none";

	
	if (document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].dispFlg")[0].value == 'true') {

		memoBlock.style.display = "";
	}

	
	if (memoBlock.style.display == "none") {
		return;
	}
	
	var editAddButton = "true";
	var editStatus = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].editStatus")[0].value;

	
	var editButton = document.getElementById("meetingMemo_EditButton_" + i);
	var cancelButton = document.getElementById("meetingMemo_CancelButton_" + i);
	var deleteButton = document.getElementById("meetingMemo_DeleteButton_" + i);

	var conferenceButton = document.getElementById("g1ConferenceButton_" + i);
	var calButton = document.getElementById("meetingMemoCalButton_" + i);
	var privateFlg = document.getElementById("privateFlg_" + i);
	
	
	
	var nameConfirmText = document.getElementById("conferenceName_ConfirmText_" + i);
	var nameEditText = document.getElementById("conferenceName_EditText_" + i);
	var detailConfirmText = document.getElementById("conferenceDetail_ConfirmText_" + i);
	var detailEditText = document.getElementById("conferenceDetail_EditText_" + i);

	
	var privateConfirm = document.getElementById("privateFlg_Confirm_" + i);
	var privateEdit = document.getElementById("privateFlg_Edit_" + i);

	if (document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].errorFlg")[0].value == '1') {
		
		
		editButton.disabled = true;
		
		cancelButton.disabled = false;
		
		deleteButton.disabled = false;

		conferenceButton.disabled = false;
		calButton.disabled = false;
		privateFlg.disabled = false;
		
		nameConfirmText.style.display = "none";
		nameEditText.style.display = "";
		detailConfirmText.style.display = "none";
		detailEditText.style.display = "";
		privateConfirm.style.display = "none";
		privateEdit.style.display = "";

	} else {
		if (editStatus == "1") {
			
			if (editAddButton == "true") {
				
				editButton.disabled = true;
				
				cancelButton.disabled = true;
				
				deleteButton.disabled = true;
			} else {
				
				editButton.disabled = false;
				
				cancelButton.disabled = true;
				
				deleteButton.disabled = false;
			}
	
			conferenceButton.disabled = true;
			calButton.disabled = true;
			privateFlg.disabled = true;
			
			nameConfirmText.style.display = "";
			nameEditText.style.display = "none";
			detailConfirmText.style.display = "";
			detailEditText.style.display = "none";
			privateConfirm.style.display = "";
			privateEdit.style.display = "none";
	
		} else if (editStatus == "2") {
			
			
			editButton.disabled = true;
			
			cancelButton.disabled = false;
			
			deleteButton.disabled = false;
	
			conferenceButton.disabled = false;
			calButton.disabled = false;
			privateFlg.disabled = false;
			
// TODO
			nameConfirmText.style.display = "none";
			nameEditText.style.display = "";
			detailConfirmText.style.display = "none";
			detailEditText.style.display = "";
			privateConfirm.style.display = "none";
			privateEdit.style.display = "";
			stretchTextArea();

		} else if (editStatus == "3") {
			
			
			memoBlock.style.display = "none";
			updateCheckInputChange("true");

		}
	}

}

	controlForMeetingMemoBlock(i);

function meetingMemoCancelButtonClick(i) {
	
	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].editStatus")[0].value = "1";
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceCodeInitValue")[0].value;
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceName")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceNameInitValue")[0].value;
	document.getElementById("conferenceName_ConfirmText_" + i).innerText = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceNameInitValue")[0].value + " ";

	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDate")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDateInitValue")[0].value;
	document.getElementById("mettingMemoDate_" + i).innerText = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDateInitDisp")[0].value;

	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDetail")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDetailInitValue")[0].value;
	document.getElementById("conferenceDetail_ConfirmText_" + i).innerText = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1ConferenceDetailInitValue")[0].value;

	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1PrivateFlg")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1PrivateFlgInitValue")[0].value;
	if (document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1PrivateFlgInitValue")[0].value == '1') {
		document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1PrivateFlg")[0].checked = true;
	} else {
		document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].g1PrivateFlg")[0].checked = false;
	}

	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].errorFlg")[0].value = '0'; 

	
	controlForMeetingMemoBlock(i);
}


function meetingMemoDeleteButtonClick(i) {
	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].editStatus")[0].value = "3";
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].dataKbn")[0].value = "3";

	
	document.getElementsByName("meetingMemoEntityList[" + i + "].errorFlg")[0].value = '0'; 

	
	controlForMeetingMemoBlock(i);
}


function privateFlgControl(i) {
	var privateFlg = document.getElementById("privateFlg_" + i);
	var privateFlgHidden = document.getElementById("g1PrivateFlgHidden_" + i);

	if (privateFlgHidden) {
		if (privateFlg.checked) {
			privateFlgHidden.value = "1";
		} else {
			privateFlgHidden.value = "0";
		}
	}
}



function buttonTagSearchClick() {

	var retVal = new Array();
	var paramAry = {};

	
	var width = '1008';
	var heigth = '520';

	
	paramAry["displayId"]  = "SYQAA860";// 画面タイトル SYQAA860=タグ付け検索
	paramAry["searchMode"] = "1";

	paramAry["g1DocumentId"] = "IN202310B06811";
	paramAry["g1DocumentCategory"] = "T";


	
	
	//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA860E00SFnd', paramAry, '', width, heigth, null, 'yes' );

	comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA860E00SFnd',paramAry,'buttonTagSearchClick',width,heigth, null, 'yes' );
	
	return false;
	

}



function buttonTagSearchClickReturn(){
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}


	
	for(var i = 0; i < retVal.length; i++) {
		if(retVal[i]) {
			retVal[i] = retVal[i].replace("&#44;", ",");
		}
	}



	
	
	

	if (retVal != undefined && (typeof(retVal[1]) != "undefined") 
	&&  (retVal[1] != "")) {

	
		
		var beforeValue = document.getElementById("addTagView").innerText;
		
		document.getElementById("addTagView").innerText = "" + beforeValue + retVal[1];


		// 再描画の用に追加したタグ名情報を再設定
		document.getElementById("additionalNoteTagListInfo").innerText = "" + beforeValue + retVal[1];


	}

	
	// 再描画しない為、なにもしない

}






function openWindowShiftedOfTagDetail(g1TagId) { 



		// タグ削除情報取得
		var tagIdList = [];
		tagIdList[0] = g1TagId;
		// タグが存在していれば処理続行、削除されていれば処理中止、削除済の場合は該当タグ名リンクを赤くする。
		if (!existAllTags(tagIdList,true,false,false)) {// tagCommon_js.jsp
			return;
		}



	var qs = {
		g1DocumentCategory: "T",
		displayId: "SYQAA880",// FTIRタグ詳細(確認)
		dispMode:  1,
		g1TagId:   g1TagId
	};
	comOpenWindowShifted('/SYQAA880E00SFnd', 'SYQAA880' + g1TagId, qs, '1008', '600', '23', '23');


}





function callCheckWarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyAndGetFtirs(samePushButtonFlg) {

	var reportVehiclesValue = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.strReportVehicles')[0].value;

	
	
	if (!integerCheck(reportVehiclesValue)) {
		reportVehiclesValue = 0;
	}
	
	
	if (document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value == '2') {

		
		if (('20231120').trim() == "") {
			

			

			getFtirs(document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession')[0].value);
			var ftirCnt = parseInt(document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeFtirs')[0].value);
			var ftirs   = parseInt(document.getElementsByName('syqu00SupersedeCountEntity.g1SupersedeVehicles')[0].value);
			
			ftirCnt = ftirCnt + 1;

			ftirs   = ftirs   + Number(reportVehiclesValue);
			
			document.getElementById("AlertMsgTd").innerText = 
				document.getElementById("AlertMsgReplace").innerText.replace("AlertMsgReplaceFtirs",
																			 callSYQU00ServiceImpGetDispFtirCountMain(ftirCnt, ftirs));
		}
		
		
		if (samePushButtonFlg == true) {
			
			warrantyToAlertNotifyFromSYQAA050Button();
		}
		
		checkWarrantyToWatchAlertNotify();
	}
}



function integerCheck(value) {

	
	if (value != value.match(/[0-9]+/g)) {
		
		return false;
	}
	
	return true;
}

	



function checkWarrantyToWatchAlertNotify() {

	var alertFtir     = "false";
	var alertWarranty = "false";
	var alertVehicle  = "false";

	var alertFtirObj     = document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertFtir')[0];
	var alertWarrantyObj = document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertWarranty')[0];
	var alertVehicleObj  = document.getElementsByName('syqu00WarrantyToWatchAlertNotifyViewEntity.alertVehicle')[0];
	
	if ((typeof(alertFtirObj) != "undefined") && (alertFtirObj != null)) {
		alertFtir     = alertFtirObj.value;
	}
	if ((typeof(alertWarrantyObj) != "undefined") && (alertWarrantyObj != null)) {
		alertWarranty = alertWarrantyObj.value;
	}
		if ((typeof(alertVehicleObj) != "undefined") && (alertVehicleObj != null)) {
		alertVehicle = alertVehicleObj.value;
	}

	var alertMsgTdFtirObj     = document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdFtir');
	var alertMsgTdWarrantyObj = document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdWarranty');
	var alertMsgTdVehicleObj = document.getElementById('AlertMsgTdVehicle');
	
	if ((typeof(alertMsgTdFtirObj) != "undefined") && (alertMsgTdFtirObj != null)) {
		if (alertFtir == "true") {
			
			alertMsgTdFtirObj.style.display     = "";
		} else {
			
			alertMsgTdFtirObj.style.display     = "none";
		}
	}
	
	if ((typeof(alertMsgTdWarrantyObj) != "undefined") && (alertMsgTdWarrantyObj != null)) {
		if (alertWarranty == "true") {
			
			alertMsgTdWarrantyObj.style.display = "";
		} else {
			
			alertMsgTdWarrantyObj.style.display = "none";
		}
	}
	
		if ((typeof(alertMsgTdVehicleObj) != "undefined") && (alertMsgTdVehicleObj != null)) {
		if (alertVehicle == "true") {
			
			alertMsgTdVehicleObj.style.display = "";
		} else {
			
			alertMsgTdVehicleObj.style.display = "none";
		}
	}
}

function dispQuickBehold(value) {

	
	if (true) {
		return;
	}

	
	if ('1' == value) {
		$('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg').disabled = false;
		siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg'));
	} else {
		$('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg').disabled = true;
		siftCtrl.changeDisable($('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg'));
		$('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg').checked = false;
		siftCtrl.changeCheck($('fpcrQuickBeholdFlg'));
	}

}




function updateEvaluationChange(idx, values) {
	document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.evaluationEntityList[" + idx + "].g1EvaluationValue")[0].value = values;
}


function resetEvaluationValue(idx) {
	for (var cntIdx = 0; cntIdx < idx; cntIdx++) {
			document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.evaluationEntityList[" + cntIdx+ "].g1EvaluationValue")[0].value = " ";

			document.getElementById('g1EvaluationValue_VB_' + cntIdx).checked = false;
			document.getElementById('g1EvaluationValue_V_' + cntIdx).checked = false;
			document.getElementById('g1EvaluationValue_N_' + cntIdx).checked = false;
			document.getElementById('g1EvaluationValue_G_' + cntIdx).checked = false;
			document.getElementById('g1EvaluationValue_VG_' + cntIdx).checked = false;

	}
}


/**
 * 複数ファイル添付チェックボックスクリック処理

}

/**
 * 添付ボタン押下処理
 */
function buttonAttachClick(inputTypeFileName) {

	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}
	// 複数添付チェックボックスが選択されていないときは	/SYQAA090ED2SFnd （単一添付）
	// 複数添付チェックボックスが選択されているときは	/SYQAA305E00SUld （複数添付）

	if( ! document.getElementById(inputTypeFileName).checked ) {

		if (comSubmit("1")) {// 2重サブミット対応
			inputCancelMsgFlg = '0'
			var form = document.forms[0];
			form.action = comContextPath + '/SYQAA090ED2SFnd.do';
			form.method = 'post';
			form.submit();
		}
	}else{
		// 本画面のFormをSessionに退避して一括添付画面で参照・更新する。
		// ダイアログ起動ではForm情報が初期化されてしまう。また、submit()の連続はサーバーでの処理順序が保証されない。Ajaxとsubmit()で画面起動する。
		if (comSubmit("1")) {// 2重サブミット対応
			new Ajax.Request( 
				comContextPath + "/SYQAA305E00SUld.do", 
				{ 
					"method": "post", 
					"parameters": Form.serialize('syqaa090Form'), 
					onSuccess: function(request) { 
						if (request == null) {
							return;
						}
						// 画面サイズ設定
						// var width = '400';
						var width = '500';
						var heigth = '600';
					
						// 画面間情報設定
						var paramAry = {};
						paramAry["parentId"] = "090";
						paramAry["ftirId"] = document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value.trim();
		
						// 複数ファイル一括添付画面 モーダル起動
						var retVal = new Array();

						//retVal = comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA305E01SUld', paramAry, this, width, heigth, null, 'no', 'no');
						comShowModalDialog('/SYQAA305E01SUld',paramAry,'attachmentFileCheckboxClick',width,heigth, null, 'no', 'no');
	
						return false;
						

						
					} 
				} 
			);
		}
	}
}




function attachmentFileCheckboxClickReturn(){
	
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}

	// 「×」ボタン押下時
	if(typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		submitFlg = true;// 次クリックを有効にする
		return;
	}
	
	// 「閉じる」ボタン
	if ( retVal == "CLOSE" ) {
		submitFlg = true;// 次クリックを有効にする
		return;
	}
	
	// 一括添付ボタン処理成功時
	if ( retVal == "0" ) {
		inputCancelMsgFlg = '0'
		var form = document.forms[0];
		form.action = comContextPath + '/SYQAA090EF8SFnd.do';
		form.method = 'post';
		form.submit();
	}
}

/**
 * 「案件No.を直接入力する」チェックボックスの表示を制御します
 *
 */
function controlAJCheckboxDisplay() {
	var elements = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ActionJudgementCode");
	for (i = elements.length; i--;) {
		if (elements[i].checked) {
			if (elements[i].value == 2
					|| elements[i].value == 3) {
				document.getElementById("insSbNoTd").style.visibility = "visible";

				document.getElementById("refBtn").disabled = false;
					
			} else {
				if (document.getElementById("insSbNoChk").checked) {
					clearAJButtonDisplay();
				}
				document.getElementById("insSbNoTd").style.visibility = "hidden";

				document.getElementById("refBtn").disabled = true;
			
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	clearAJButtonDisplay();
}

/**
 * 「参照」「入力」ボタンの切り替えを制御します
 */
function controlAJButtonDisplay(checkBoxId, dispButtonId, nonDispButtonId) {
	if (document.getElementById(checkBoxId).checked) {
		changeObjDisplay(dispButtonId, nonDispButtonId);
	} else {
		changeObjDisplay(nonDispButtonId, dispButtonId);
	}
}

/**
 * 2つのオブジェクトの表示・非表示を同時に切り替えます
 */
function changeObjDisplay(dispObjId, nonDispObjId) {
	document.getElementById(dispObjId).style.display = "inline";
	document.getElementById(nonDispObjId).style.display = "none";
}

/**
 * 「案件No.を直接入力する」チェックのクリアと非表示化
 * 「参照」「入力」ボタンの切り替えを制御
 */
function clearAJButtonDisplay() {
	document.getElementById("insSbNoChk").checked = false;
	document.getElementById("insSbNoTd").style.visibility = "hidden";
	changeObjDisplay("refBtn", "insBtn");
}

/**
 * 案件No.入力の子画面を開く
 */
function searchBundingPuttingDirect() {
	/** 2重サブミット防止 */
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return;
	}
	
	/** 既に案件検索ウィンドウが存在する場合、ウィンドウを閉じる */
	closeAnotherWindow("SYQAA120" + "IN202310B06811");

	var g1ConductionBaseCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ConductionBaseCode").value;

	var g1ManufactureFactoryCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ManufactureFactoryCode").value;
	if (g1ManufactureFactoryCode.trim() == "") {
		alert('YQ10112E: You need to set the production base on SEARCH FOR THE SUPERSESSION. Input it.');
		return;
	}

	var g1ProductCategory = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ProductCategory").value;

	var doneFlg = "0"

	/** クエリストリング生成 */
	var qs = {
		g1ConductionBaseCode : g1ConductionBaseCode,
		g1ManufactureFactoryCode : g1ManufactureFactoryCode,
		g1ProductCategory : g1ProductCategory,
		doneFlg : doneFlg
	};

	var windowName = 'SYQAA890' + 'IN202310B06811';

	/** 呼出先画面サイズ設定 */
	var width = '750';
	var heigth = '275';

	comOpenWindow('/SYQAA890E00SFnd', windowName, qs, width, heigth);
}

/**
 * ウィンドウ名に該当するウィンドウをクローズします
 */
function closeAnotherWindow(windowName) {
	var windowObj = window.open("", windowName.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,""), "");
	if (windowObj && !windowObj.closed) {
		windowObj.close();
	}
}


/**
 * 処置判断理由欄を制御する
 */
function controlAJReasonDisplay() {
	var ajCode = document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementCode").value;
	if (ajCode == 4){
		changeObjDisplay("ajReasonEnd", "ajReasonNotEnd");
	} else {
		changeObjDisplay("ajReasonNotEnd", "ajReasonEnd");
	}
}
/**
 * 処置判断理由テキストエリア本体の更新
 */
function updAJReasonTextArea(changedArea) {
	var changedValue = document.getElementById(changedArea).value;
	
	if(changedArea == 'ajReasonEn' || changedArea == 'ajReasonEn_2') {
		// 本体へ反映
		document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementReasonEn').value = changedValue;
		// 表示用へ反映
		document.getElementById('ajReasonEn').value = changedValue;
		document.getElementById('ajReasonEn_2').value = changedValue;
		
	} else if(changedArea == 'ajReasonPl' || changedArea == 'ajReasonPl_2') {
		// 本体へ反映
		document.getElementById('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity_g1ActionJudgementReasonPl').value = changedValue;
		// 表示用へ反映
		document.getElementById('ajReasonPl').value = changedValue;
		document.getElementById('ajReasonPl_2').value = changedValue;
	}
}

function translationUpdateConfirm(){
	//翻訳対象欄に値が入力されている場合、確認ウインドウを出す
	var translationUpdFlg = false;

	//チェック対象オブジェクト
	var targetElements = new Array();
	
	//添付ファイルの要素数
	var attachmentCntElement = document.getElementsByName("troubleSituationAttachmentCnt")[0];
	var attachmentCnt = attachmentCntElement?.value;

	//翻訳言語
	var translationLanguage = document.getElementsByName("translationLanguage")[0].value.split(":");
	
	if (translationLanguage[0] == "English"){
	// 英語→ユーザー言語への翻訳の場合
		
		//基本情報ブロック
		//件名(PL)
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectPl")[0]);
		//不具合状況ブロック
		//不具合内容・件名
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultSubjectTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・お客様の申し出
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultProposalTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・どうなったか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultBecameTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・どんな点検をしたか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultCheckTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・点検結果どうだったか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultResultTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・何が原因か
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultCauseTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・なぜそう判断したのか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultWhyTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・処置内容
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultDisposalContentsTl")[0]);

		//続報
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FollowUpTl")[0]);

		
		//添付ファイルのコメント
		if(attachmentCnt > 0){
			for(var i = 0; i < attachmentCnt; i++){
				targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.syqaa090FindWebAttachmentEntity[" + i + "].g1Comment")[0]);
			}
		}
	
	}else{
	// ユーザー言語→英語への翻訳の場合

		//基本情報ブロック
		//件名(En)
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SubjectEn")[0]);
		//不具合状況ブロック
		//不具合内容・件名
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultSubjectTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・お客様の申し出
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultProposalTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・どうなったか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultBecameTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・どんな点検をしたか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultCheckTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・点検結果どうだったか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultResultTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・何が原因か
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultCauseTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・なぜそう判断したのか
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultWhyTl")[0]);
		//不具合内容・処置内容
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FaultDisposalContentsTl")[0]);

		//続報
		targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1FollowUpTl")[0]);


		//添付ファイルのコメント
		if(attachmentCnt > 0){
			for(var i = 0; i < attachmentCnt; i++){
				targetElements.push(document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.syqaa090FindWebAttachmentEntity[" + i + "].g1Comment")[0]);
			}
		}

	}

	//翻訳結果の出力先をチェックして、空欄かどうかを確認する
	for (var i = 0; i < targetElements.length; i++ ){
		var target = targetElements[i];
		if(target?.value !== ""){
			translationUpdFlg = true;
		}
	}

	//翻訳結果の出力先が空欄ではない場合はメッセージを表示する
	if(translationUpdFlg){
		if(comConfirm('The translation has already been completed.\r\nDo you want to overwrite the contents?')){
			if (comSubmit("1")) {
				document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EY1SUpd.do";
				document.forms[0].submit();
			}
		}else{
			return false;
		}
	}else{
		if (comSubmit("1")) {
			document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EY1SUpd.do";
			document.forms[0].submit();
		}
	}
}





function importInvestigationResult(){
		if (!submitFlg) {
			return false;
		}
		if (comSubmit("1")) {// 2重サブミット対応
				inputCancelMsgFlg = '0'
				var form = document.forms[0];
				form.action = comContextPath + '/SYQAA090EY2SFnd.do';
				form.method = 'post';
				form.submit();
		}
}	


/**
* return関数を制御する関数
*/

function doReturnFunction(funcNm) {
	document.body.style.cursor = 'auto';
	if (funcNm == "openAssginWindow0") {
		openAssginWindowReturn(0);
	}　else if (funcNm == "openAssginWindow") {
		openAssginWindowReturn(1);
	} else if (funcNm == "openSubAssginWindow0") {
		openSubAssginWindowReturn(0);
	} else if (funcNm == "openSubAssginWindow") {
		openSubAssginWindowReturn(1);
	} else if (funcNm == "showSearch") {
		showSearchReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "showSearch3") {
		showSearch3Return();
	} else if (funcNm == "openInputSupportWindow") {
		openInputSupportWindowReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "onCloseSYQAA090") {
		onCloseSYQAA090Return();
	} else if (funcNm == "checkCompleteWithoutFlg") {
		checkCompleteWithoutFlgReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "attachmentFileCheckboxClick") {
		attachmentFileCheckboxClickReturn();
	}　else if (funcNm == "buttonTagSearchClick") {
		buttonTagSearchClickReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "checkComplete") {
		checkCompleteReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "checkCompleteReturn") {
		checkCompleteReturn2(true);
	} else if (funcNm == "checkCompleteMytask") {
		checkCompleteMytaskReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "checkCompleteMytaskReturn") {
		checkCompleteMytaskReturn2(true);
	} else if (funcNm == "openFolder") {
		openFolderReturn();

	} else if (funcNm == "sbIdRank") {
		sbIdRankReturn();


	} else if (funcNm == "notificationMethodWindow") {
		notificationMethodReturn();
	} else if (funcNm == "collectionDestinationWindow") {
		collectionDestinationReturn();

	}
}





function redirectToProcessBlock(){
	var processCode = "";
	var proccessCodeExtension = "";

	//基本情報より下のブロックのY座標
	var divPosition = $("divMAIN").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	//対象プロセスブロックのY座標
	var processPosition;
	
	if(processCode == "110"){
		processPosition = $("#Acceptance").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "123"){
		processPosition = $("#Thing").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "130"){
		processPosition = $("#InformationCollection").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "500"){
		processPosition = $("#top_process_" + 500 + "_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "135"){
		processPosition = $("#IBCR_RAISE_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "137"){
		processPosition = $("#IBCR_RAISEWf_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "140"){
		processPosition = $("#Ci_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "147"){
		processPosition = $("#InvestigationWf_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "150"){
		processPosition = $("#TreatmentJudgment").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "160"){
		processPosition = $("#TreatmentJudgment").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "330"){
		processPosition = $("#Feedback").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "340"){
		processPosition = $("#FeedbackWf").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "520"){
		processPosition = $("#Tsr").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "510"){
		processPosition = $("#Mytask_" + proccessCodeExtension).getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else if(processCode == "15Z"){
		processPosition = $("TreatmentJudgmentJustComlete").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	
	}else{
		processPosition = $("#ReportTop").getBoundingClientRect().top;
	}
	
	if(document.getElementsByName('scrollY')[0].value == ""){
		document.getElementById("divMAIN").scrollTop = processPosition - divPosition;
	}else{
		comSetScroll();
	}
	
	return;
}





function stretchTextArea(){
	
	//height取得のため非表示エリアを一旦表示させる
	var targetAry = [];
	var objAry = document.getElementsByTagName("div");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var targetDiv = objAry[i];
		if(targetDiv.style.display == "none"){
			targetAry.push(targetDiv);
		}
	}
	
	for(var i = 0; i < targetAry.length; i++){
		var targetDiv = targetAry[i];
		targetDiv.style.display = "";
	}
	
	objAry = document.getElementsByTagName("textarea");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var target = objAry[i];
		
		//スクロールバーを非表示に
		target.style.overflow = "hidden";
		
		//リサイズ不可
		target.style.resize = "none";
		
		//高さの調整
		var targetHeight = target.style.height;
		targetHeight = targetHeight.replace("px", "");
		if(targetHeight < target.scrollHeight || targetHeight == ""){
			target.style.height = [target.scrollHeight, "px"].join("");
		}
		targetHeight = target.style.height;
		targetHeight = targetHeight.replace("px", "");
		if(targetHeight < 17){
			target.style.height = "17px";
		}
	}
	
	//テキストエリアにinputイベントを付与して動的に高さを調整するように
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var target = objAry[i];
		target.addEventListener("input",function(){
			//改行削除時にズレが生じないように一度基準値にリセットする
			if(parseInt(this.style.height) > 17){
				this.style.height = "17";
			}	
			this.style.height = [this.scrollHeight, "px"].join("");
		});
	}
	
	//表示させたエリアを再度非表示にする
	for(var i = 0; i < targetAry.length; i++){
		var targetDiv = targetAry[i];
		targetDiv.style.display = "none";
	}
	
	return;
}


function stretchInputFile(){
	//height取得のため非表示エリアを一旦表示させる
	var targetAry = [];
	var objAry = document.getElementsByTagName("div");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var targetDiv = objAry[i];
		if(targetDiv.style.display == "none"){
			targetAry.push(targetDiv);
		}
	}
	
	//IE以外ではファイル選択ボタンがheight26以下だと見切れるため調整する
	var inputAry = [];
	objAry = document.getElementsByTagName("input");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var targetInput = objAry[i];
		if(targetInput.type == "file"){
			inputAry.push(targetInput);
		}
	}
	
	for(var i = 0; i < inputAry.length; i++){
		var targetInput = inputAry[i];
		var targetHeight = targetInput.style.height;
		targetHeight = targetHeight.replace("px", "");
		if(targetHeight < 26){
			targetInput.style.height = "26px";
		}
	}
	
	//表示させたエリアを再度非表示にする
	for(var i = 0; i < targetAry.length; i++){
		var targetDiv = targetAry[i];
		targetDiv.style.display = "none";
	}
	
	return;
}


function waterMarkForEdge(){
	var objAry = document.getElementsByClassName("WATERMARK");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var target = objAry[i];
		target.style.opacity = "20%";
		target.style.top = "-3px";
	}
	
	objAry = document.getElementsByClassName("WATERMARKR");
	for(i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var target = objAry[i];
		target.previousElementSibling.style.height = "31px";
		target.style.opacity = "70%";
		target.style.top = "-17px";
	}
}



function clearDisplayBlock(){
	var objAry = document.getElementsByTagName("TR");
	for(var i = 0; i < objAry.length; i++){
		var target = objAry[i];
		if(target.style.display == "block"){
			target.style.display = "";
		}
	}
}

function gotoLink(id) {
	
	//基本情報より下のブロックのY座標
	var divPosition = $("divMAIN").getBoundingClientRect().top;

	//対象プロセスブロックのY座標
	var processPosition = $(id).getBoundingClientRect().top;

	if(document.getElementsByName('scrollY')[0].value == ""){

		document.getElementById("divMAIN").scrollTop = document.getElementById("divMAIN").scrollTop + processPosition - divPosition;

	}else{
		comSetScroll();
	}
	
	return;
}

function setAttachmentBrowsingControlAllStat(){
	
	
	
	let chkbox_all = document.getElementById('attachment_browsing_control_all');
	if (chkbox_all){
		let chkBoxElements = document.getElementsByClassName("AttachmentBrowsingControlCheckBox");
	
		let chkBoxAllStat = true;
		
		
		for (i = 0; i < chkBoxElements.length; i++ ){
			if(chkBoxElements[i].checked){
				continue;
			}
			
			chkBoxAllStat = false;
			break;
		}
		
		
		chkbox_all.checked = chkBoxAllStat;
	}

}

function changeAttachmentBrowsingControlAll(){
	
	
	let chkBoxElements = document.getElementsByClassName("AttachmentBrowsingControlCheckBox");
	let chkBoxAllStat = document.getElementById('attachment_browsing_control_all').checked;
	
	
	for (i = 0; i < chkBoxElements.length; i++ ){
		chkBoxElements[i].checked =  chkBoxAllStat;
		changeinvalidFlgEdited(i);
	}

}




function sbIdRankReturn() {
	
	var sbId = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1SbIdSupersession")[0].value;
	
	var ajaxOptions = {
			asynchronous: false,
			method: "post",
			evalScripts: true,
			parameters: "key=" + sbId, 
			onComplete: callBackSbIdRank
		}
	
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/SYQAA090E94SFnd.do", ajaxOptions);
}

function callBackSbIdRank(request) {
	
	var retVal = request.responseText;
	
	if (retVal != "") {
	
		var sbIdRankArray = eval("(" + retVal + ")"); 
		
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090Form_g1RankSecondaryJudgement").value = sbIdRankArray.g1RankJudgement;
		document.getElementById("SYQAA090Form_g1SupersedeFtirs").value = sbIdRankArray.g1SupersedeFtirs;

		var sbIdMessage = "supersession destination FPCR (SBPR) ---- FTIRs: <newFtirs> ／ Rank: <newRankJudgement>";

		sbIdMessage = sbIdMessage.replace("<newFtirs>", sbIdRankArray.g1SupersedeFtirs);
		sbIdMessage = sbIdMessage.replace("<newRankJudgement>", sbIdRankArray.g1RankJudgement);
		
		document.getElementById("syqaa090_sbIdRank").innerText = sbIdMessage;
		document.getElementById("syqaa090_sbIdRank").style.display = "";
		
	}
}



function openFolder(folderType) {

		var paramAry = {};
		
		var folderPath;
		if (folderType == '1') {

			folderPath = document.getElementsByName('folderPath')[0].value;
			var ibcrPath = document.getElementsByName('ibcrFolderPath')[0].value;
			
			if (folderPath.length > 0 && ibcrPath.length > 0) {
				//所属拠点コード
				var userBaseCode = document.getElementsByName('userBaseCode')[0].value;
				//IBCR元拠点コード
				var conductionBaseCode = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode')[0].value;
				
				//拠点コードからメッセージキーの引き当てを行う (辞書配列はSYQAA090_js.jspのbaseCodeKeysに定義)
				var btnBase = baseCodeKeys[userBaseCode];		//自拠点
				var btnIbcr = baseCodeKeys[conductionBaseCode];	//IBCR元拠点
				var paramAry = {};
				paramAry["messageId"] = "YQ10480I";
				paramAry["buttonId"] = btnBase + "," + btnIbcr + "," + btnCancel;

				var returnAry = [];
				returnAry.push(btnBase, btnIbcr, conductionBaseCode);
				
				//ファイルプロトコルONの場合はリンクを後続処理に渡して無効化(aタグの挙動抑制)
				if (folderPath.indexOf('file://')　!= -1) {
					var folderLink = document.getElementById('workFolderLink').getAttribute('href');
					document.getElementById('workFolderLink').setAttribute('href','javaScript:void(0)');
					returnAry.push(folderLink);
				}
	
				document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();
				
				comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, 'openFolder', 460, 150);
				
				return false;
				
			}
			if (folderPath.length == 0) {
				folderPath = ibcrPath;
				//ファイルプロトコルONの場合はプラグイン経由でエクスプローラーを開く
				if (folderPath.indexOf('file://')　!= -1) {
					folderPath = document.getElementById('ibcrFolderLink').getAttribute('href');
					window.location = folderPath;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (ibcrPath.length > 0) {
				paramAry["ibcrBaseCode"] = document.getElementsByName('syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1ConductionBaseCode')[0].value;
			}
			
		} else {
			folderPath = document.getElementsByName('relatedMaterialFolderPath')[0].value;
		}
		
		//ファイルプロトコルONの場合はプラグイン経由でエクスプローラーを開く
		if (folderPath.indexOf('file://')　!= -1) {
			return;
		}
		
		
		var ftirNo = document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value;
	
		paramAry["documentId"] = ftirNo;
		paramAry["folderType"] = folderType;	
	
		
		var width = '1010';
		var heigth = '800';
		var top = '23';
		var left = '23';
	
		comOpenWindow('/SYQAA950E00SFnd','SYQAA950' + ftirNo + folderType,paramAry,width,heigth,top,left);
		
		return false;
}




function openFolderReturn(){
	var retVal;
	var folderPath;
	
	var paramAry = {};
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
	
	var btnBase = returnAry[0];　//自拠点
	var btnIbcr = returnAry[1];	//IBCR元拠点
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	
	switch (retVal[0]) {
		case btnBase:
			folderPath = document.getElementsByName('folderPath')[0].value;
			//ファイルプロトコルONの場合はプラグイン経由でエクスプローラーを開く
			if (folderPath.indexOf('file://')　!= -1) {
				document.getElementById('workFolderLink').setAttribute('href', returnAry[3]);
				folderPath = document.getElementById('workFolderLink').getAttribute('href');
				window.location = folderPath;
				return;
			}
			break;
		case btnIbcr:
			folderPath = document.getElementsByName('ibcrFolderPath')[0].value;
			//ファイルプロトコルONの場合はプラグイン経由でエクスプローラーを開く
			if (folderPath.indexOf('file://')　!= -1) {
				folderPath = document.getElementById('ibcrFolderLink').getAttribute('href');
				window.location = folderPath;
				return;
			}
			paramAry["ibcrBaseCode"] = returnAry[2];
			break;
		case btnCancel:	
			return false;
		default:
			break;
	}

	
	if (typeof(retVal) == "undefined") {
		document.body.style.cursor = "default";
		return false;
	}
	
	
	var ftirNo = document.getElementsByName("g1FtirId")[0].value;
	var folderType = '1';

	paramAry["documentId"] = ftirNo;
	paramAry["folderType"] = folderType;

	
	var width = '1010';
	var heigth = '800';
	var top = '23';
	var left = '23';

	comOpenWindow('/SYQAA950E00SFnd','SYQAA950' + ftirNo + folderType,paramAry,width,heigth,top,left);

	return true;
}

function removeReadOnly() {
	oElements = document.getElementsByClassName("UsageConditionInput");
	for (i = 0; i < oElements.length; i++) {
		if (oElements[i].classList.contains('TEXT_READONLY')) {
			oElements[i].classList.remove('TEXT_READONLY');
		}	
	}
}

		} else {

		}
	}

function updCollectionStatus() {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}

	var retVal;
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(",");
	}

	
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionRequestStatus").value = retVal.shift();			// 回収要求ステータス
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionDestination").value = retVal.shift();				// 回収現品送付先
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionDestinationName").value = retVal.shift();			// 回収現品送付先
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionNotificationMethodCode").value = retVal.shift();	// 通知方法

	
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionRequestNotification").value = retVal.join(",");	// 回収依頼時特記事項
	
	document.forms[0].action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090E40SUpd.do";
	document.forms[0].submit();

}


function noNeedSendCancelClick() {

	
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}
	
	
	let confirmMsg = 'YQ10491W: Are you sure to cancel the collection not required?';
	
	
	if (!comConfirm(confirmMsg)) {
			return false;
		}

	// 回収要求処理コードをセット
	document.getElementById("syqaa090FindTFtirViewEntity.g1CollectionProcessCode").value = '4';

	return true;

}

}

<SCRIPT type="text/javascript">

<!--

/**
 * ドロップダウンリスト切り替え時のマイタスク名入力欄の制御
 * @param target 対象のドロップダウンリスト
 * @param index  行インデックス
 */
function onChangeG1MyTaskCode(target, index) {
	var elmMyTaskName = document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity_" + index + "_g1MytaskName");
	var elmMyTaskNameDisabled = document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity_" + index + "_g1MytaskNameDisabled");
	if (elmMyTaskNameDisabled.value == 'false'
			&& target.value == "99") {
		elmMyTaskName.readOnly = false;
		elmMyTaskName.className = elmMyTaskName.className.replace(/TEXT_READONLY/g, "");
	} else {
		elmMyTaskName.readOnly = true;
		elmMyTaskName.className += " TEXT_READONLY";
	}
}

/**
 * 作業･報告待ち状態チェックのクリック時の作業・報告待ち日の制御
 * @param target 対象のチェックボックス
 * @param index  行インデックス
 */
function onClickG1WaitingFlg(target, index) {
	var waitingDateId = "syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity_" + index + "_g1WaitingDate";
	onChangeWaitingFlg(target, "out_" + waitingDateId, waitingDateId, 'en_US');
}

/**
 * 作業・報告待ち日の変更時の作業・報告待ち状態チェックの制御
 * @param target 対象のチェックボックス
 * @param index  行インデックス
 */
function onChangeWaitingDate(target, index) {
	var elmWaitingDate = document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity_" + index + "_g1WaitingDate");
	var elmWaitingFlg = document.getElementById("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntity_" + index + "_g1WaitingFlg");
	if (elmWaitingDate.value.replace(/\s+/g, "") != "") {
		elmWaitingFlg.checked = true;
	} else {
		elmWaitingFlg.checked = false;
	}
}



// 作業･報告待ち状態チェックを判定して、作業･報告待ち日に日付をセットします
function onChangeWaitingFlg( element, dataId, dataHeddenId, langCode ) {

	// ﾁｪｯｸ判定
	if ( element.checked != true ) {
		// ﾁｪｯｸなし
		document.getElementById(dataId).innerHTML   = '';
		document.getElementById(dataHeddenId).value = '';
		return;
	}

	// 本日、１週間後の日付編集
	var nowNextDate = new Date(); 
	nowNextDate.setDate(nowNextDate.getDate() + 7);

	// 日付フォーマット編集
	var waitingDate = "";
	if (langCode && langCode.length >= 2
		&& (langCode.substring(0, 2) == "ja"
			|| langCode.substring(0, 2) == "zh")){ // 国コードも含む形式(ja-JP)での指定も考慮が必要
		// 日本語・中国語形式の場合、"YYYY.MM.DD"形式
		waitingDate = 'YYYY.MM.DD';
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/YYYY/g, nowNextDate.getFullYear());
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/MM/g, ('0' + (nowNextDate.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2));
	  	waitingDate = waitingDate.replace(/DD/g, ('0' + nowNextDate.getDate()).slice(-2));
	} else {
		// 日本語・中国語形式以外の場合
		waitingDate = nowNextDate.getDate() + "." + monthEng[parseInt(nowNextDate.getMonth(), 10)] + "." + nowNextDate.getFullYear();
	}
	
	// 該当項目に設定
	document.getElementById(dataId).innerHTML   = waitingDate;
	document.getElementById(dataHeddenId).value = 
		nowNextDate.getFullYear() 
			+ ('0' + (nowNextDate.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) 
			+ ('0' + nowNextDate.getDate()).slice(-2);
}




// 完了ボタン押下時の変更チェック

function checkCompleteMytask(mst_kbn, filterBaseCode) {


	// 既にサブミット中の場合処理中止
	if (!submitFlg) {
		return false;
	}


	// 拠点の指定がないときは担当者・部署を選択していないと判断する
	if (filterBaseCode == undefined || filterBaseCode == '') {
		// 完了の対象プロセスがFTIRのマイタスクのとき担当者未指定として処理を行わない
		
		alert('YQ10396E: Cannot complete it for it is without the person in charge of My Task.');
		document.body.style.cursor = 'auto';
		return false;
	}


	var person = "";
	document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '0';

	// 現時点では承認者のプロセスアサインチェックを行うかわからないのでチェックを行っていないとしておく
	document.getElementsByName("isCheckedApprovalPersonProcessAssign")[0].value = '0';

	// 部署アサインかどうか
	if (document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + mst_kbn + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargeDeptCode")[0].value.trim() != ""
		&& document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + mst_kbn + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value.trim() == "") {
		// 部署がアサインされている
		person = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
		document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + mst_kbn + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;
		document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
	} else {
		// 人がアサインされている
		person = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + mst_kbn + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0].value;
	}

	// 担当者をログイン者に戻す（変更する）場合はtrueをセットする
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	if (document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value != person.trim()) {

		
		var paramAry = {};
		paramAry["messageId"] = "YQ10028W";
		paramAry["buttonId"] = btnYes + "," + btnNo + "," + btnCancel;
		
		
		var returnAry = [];
		returnAry.push(mst_kbn, filterBaseCode);
		
		document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value = returnAry.join();
		
		comShowModalDialog("/CommonDialog", paramAry, 'checkCompleteMytask', 460, 150);
		return false;
		

	} else {
		isPersonChanged2 = false;		
	}
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
		
	checkCompleteMytaskReturn2(false)
}



function checkCompleteMytaskReturn(){
	
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	
	var returnAry = document.getElementsByName("returnAry")[0].value.split(',');
	
	var mst_kbn = returnAry[0];
	var filterBaseCode = returnAry[1];
	
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	
	// ログインユーザと指定された担当者が違う場合は確認ダイアログを表示する
	//switch (comConfirmYesNoCancel('YQ10028W: The person in charge of this job process is another than the one (yourself) who is now completing this. Will you change the charged person of this to the one (yourself)?')) {
	switch (retVal[0]) {
		case btnYes:


			
			if (filterBaseCode != undefined && filterBaseCode != '14') {
				alert('YQ10282E: You cannot replace the person in charge and complete this job process because you belong to a different base from him/ her.');
				document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
				return false;
			}

			
			returnAssignUserFlgElement.value = '1';
			chargePersonCodeElement = document.getElementsByName("syqaa090MytaskInfoViewEntityList[" + mst_kbn + "].mytaskChargePersonViewEntity.g1ChargePersonCode")[0];
			orgAssignUserIdElement.value = chargePersonCodeElement.value;
			chargePersonCodeElement.value = document.getElementsByName("userId")[0].value;

			document.getElementsByName("param3")[0].value = '1';
			break;
		case btnNo:
			
			isPersonChanged = false;
			break;
		case btnCancel:
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			return false;
		default:
			break;
	}
	
	document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
		
	checkCompleteMytaskReturn2(false);
}
	


function checkCompleteMytaskReturn2(previousDepartmentFlg){
	
	
	var isPersonChanged = true;
	var isPersonChanged2 = true;
	var chargePersonCodeElement;
	var returnAssignUserFlgElement = new Object();
	var orgAssignUserIdElement = new Object();
	
	var retVal;
	
	if (document.getElementById("modalRetVal")) {
		retVal = document.getElementById("modalRetVal").value.split(',');
	}
	
	if(previousDepartmentFlg) {
		//switch (comConfirmYesNoCancel('YQ10040W: You can transact it as a member of your ex-Dgt. Select "Yes" when as the ex-Dgt, select "No" when as the current Dgt or select "Cancel" when you cancel it.', 'Assign')) {
		switch (retVal[0]) {
		case btnYes:
			
			document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '1';
			break;
		case btnNo:
			
			document.getElementsByName("param1")[0].value = '0';
			break;
		case btnCancel:
			document.body.style.cursor = "default";
			if (isPersonChanged && isPersonChanged2) {		
				
				chargePersonCodeElement.value = orgAssignUserIdElement.value;
			}
			return false;
		default:
			break;
		}
	}
	document.syqaa090Form.action = comContextPath + "/SYQAA090EP3SUpd.do";
	document.syqaa090Form.submit();
}

function setEditFlg(index) {

	elmEditFlg.value = '1';
}

<SCRIPT type="text/javascript">
//<--
// TableのTR背景色を変更します。
// param	row 対象TR
// param	color 変更色の値
function changeTRColor(row,color){
	for(var idxCell = 0; idxCell < row.cells.length; idxCell ++){
		var cell = row.cells.item(idxCell);// セルの取得
		cell.style.backgroundColor = color;
	}
}

// タグの存在・不在をbooleanで返します。
// 不在の場合は、背景色を赤くします。
// param	tagIdList	検査対象タグIDの配列
// param	isAlert		trueの場合は削除メッセージがAlert。falseの場合はConfirm。
// param	isTR		trueの場合は該当行が赤くなる。falseの場合は該当タグ名リンクが赤くなる。nullの場合は赤くするところがない。
// param	isDeleteBtn	trueの場合はタグ付け検索画面のタグ削除ボタンに特有の処理を行う。
// return	true:検査対象タグがすべて存在している。またはダイアログで処理続行が選択された。false:検査対象タグが一部でも削除されている。
function existAllTags(tagIdList,isAlert,isTR,isDeleteBtn){

		var ret;

		// タグ削除情報取得
		var tagInfo = getTagInfo(tagIdList);

		// 背景色変更
		for(var idxAbsentList = 0; idxAbsentList < tagInfo.absentList.length; idxAbsentList ++){
			//タグが削除されていたら、背景色を赤くする。
			if (isTR == null){
				// nullの場合は赤くするところがない。
			} else if (isTR){
				// 行を赤くする。
				changeTRColor(document.getElementById("tagId" + tagInfo.absentList[idxAbsentList].g1TagId),"RED");
			} else {
				// タグ名リンクを赤くする。
				document.getElementById("tagId" + tagInfo.absentList[idxAbsentList].g1TagId).style.backgroundColor = "RED";
			}
		}

		if (tagInfo.absentList.length > 0){
			// タグ削除済時のメッセージ表示
			if (isAlert){
				// 処理続行の選択肢はない。
				var tagName = tagInfo.absentList[0].g1TagName;
				// YQ10422E=YQ10422E:「{0}」タグは既に削除されています。
				comAlert('YQ10422E: The tag of "' + tagName + '" has already been deleted.',"",VB_ICON_CRITICAL);
				ret = false; // 処理中止
			} else {
				// 続行・中止はユーザーが選択する。
				// YQ10424W=既に削除されているタグがあります。\r\n確認せずに処理を進めますか？
				ret = comConfirm('One or more tags have already been deleted from this FTIR. \r\nAre you sure to tag this FTIR without confirming them?');
			}
		} else {
			// タグが削除されていない場合
			if (isDeleteBtn){
				// タグ削除ボタンのときだけ。ダイアログで続行・中止選択。
				var tagName = tagInfo.existentList[0].g1TagName;

				var tagDocuments = tagInfo.existentList[0].g1TagDocuments;
				if (tagDocuments > 0){
					if("" == "T"){ // FTIR
						// YQ10421W=「{0}」タグは{1}件のFTIRにタグ付けされています。\n本当に削除してよろしいですか？
						ret = comConfirm('The tag of \"' + tagName + '\" is tagged to ' + tagDocuments + ' FTIRs.\nAre you sure to delete it?');
					}else if("" == "S"){ // 案件
						// YQ10437W=「{0}」タグは{1}件の案件にタグ付けされています。\n本当に削除してよろしいですか？
						ret = comConfirm('The tag of \"' + tagName + '\" is tagged to ' + tagDocuments + ' SBPRs.\nAre you sure to delete it?');
					}

				} else {
					// YQ10420W=「{0}」タグを削除します。よろしいですか？
					ret = comConfirm('The tag of \"' + tagName + '\" is going to be deleted.\nAre you sure to delete it?');
				}
			} else {
				ret = true; // 処理続行
			}
		}
		if (!ret){
			// 処理中止の場合は、カーソル形状を通常に戻す
			document.body.style.cursor = 'auto';
		}
		return ret;
}

// タグの存在・不在情報をJSONで返します。
// param tagIdList 検査対象タグIDの配列
function getTagInfo(tagIdList){
	var tagIds = "tagIds=" + tagIdList[0];//画面仕様により、tagIdListの要素数が０の場合は考慮無用。
	for(var cnt = 1; cnt < tagIdList.length; cnt ++){
		tagIds = tagIds + "&tagIds=" + tagIdList[cnt] ;
	}
	var retVal;
	var ajaxOptions = {
		asynchronous: false,
		method: "post",
		evalScripts: true,
		parameters: tagIds,
		onComplete: function(request){
			if (request == null) {
				retVal = '';
			}
			retVal = request.responseText;
		}
	}
	new Ajax.Request(comContextPath + "/SYQAA860E07SFnd.do", ajaxOptions);
	var ｔagInfo="";
	if (retVal != "") {
		ｔagInfo = eval("(" + retVal + ")"); // 戻り値が連想配列に置き換わる
	}
	return ｔagInfo;
}
